# ND circuit diagram symbol catalogue

Every part in ND-13.003.01 "Circuit Diagram Symbols" (A/S Norsk Data-Elektronikk, July 1976), in the manual's order. Each entry shows the cut-out from the manual. Gate symbols also show the description used to recognise them on a drawing, the manual's formula, and the same gate drawn again in ND style and in modern style. Both drawings end their pin lines on the same points (checked by the unit test `GateShapeTests`).

This page is generated by `tools/build-catalog-page.py`. Do not edit it by hand.

128 parts, 31 of them gate symbols.

## How ND draws gates

| Kind | ND 1976 | Modern | How to recognise the ND symbol |
|---|---|---|---|
| NAND | ![nand2 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/nand2.png) | ![nand2 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/nand2.png) | Narrow upright rectangle, empty |
| NAND, open collector | ![nand-oc2 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/nand-oc2.png) | ![nand-oc2 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/nand-oc2.png) | The same with a vertical line down the centre |
| NOR | ![nor2 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/nor2.png) | ![nor2 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/nor2.png) | Rectangle with an X corner to corner |
| NOT | ![not1 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/not1.png) | ![not1 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/not1.png) | Rectangle with a triangle filling it |
| NOT, open collector | ![not-oc1 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/not-oc1.png) | ![not-oc1 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/not-oc1.png) | Rectangle with one diagonal, bottom left to top right |
| AND | ![and2 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/and2.png) | ![and2 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/and2.png) | One rectangle split down the middle; the right half holds the triangle |
| AND, open collector | ![and-oc2 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/and-oc2.png) | ![and-oc2 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/and-oc2.png) | Split rectangle; the right half holds one diagonal from bottom left to the middle of the right edge |
| OR | ![or2 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/or2.png) | ![or2 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/or2.png) | Split rectangle; X in the left half, triangle in the right half |
| XOR | ![xor2 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/xor2.png) | ![xor2 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/xor2.png) | Rectangle with a centre line and a short = across it |
| Schmitt trigger | ![nand-schmitt4 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/nand-schmitt4.png) | ![nand-schmitt4 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/nand-schmitt4.png) | Top edge runs on to the right, bottom edge runs on to the left |
| Buffer | ![nand-buf2 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/nand-buf2.png) | ![nand-buf2 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/nand-buf2.png) | The right end is a point |
| Line driver | ![line-driver1 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/line-driver1.png) | ![line-driver1 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/line-driver1.png) | Large open triangle |

Marks that change a symbol: a centre line, a bar before the point or a single diagonal = open collector (in modern drawings, a diamond with a bar under it; HV beside it = high-voltage open collector, which stands up to 30 V or 15 V on its pull-up instead of 5.5 V - HV is our own label); a pointed right end = buffer/driver; edges running past the box = Schmitt trigger: an input with two switching points (about 1.7 V going up, 0.9 V going down) so a slow or noisy signal gives one clean change instead of flicker. Signal subscripts: 1 = active high, 0 = active low (manual page 2).

### Where the modern marks come from

The small marks inside the modern drawings come from IEEE Std 91-1984 / IEC 617-12, as explained in Texas Instruments' *Overview of IEEE Std 91-1984* (document SDYZ001A, checked 2026-11-23):

- **Triangle** (pointing in the signal direction): "a buffer or element with more than usual output capability" (general qualifying symbols table). Used here on 7428, 7433, 7437, 7438 and 7440.
- **Diamond with a bar under it**: open-collector output, one of the "symbols inside the outline".
- **Step shape**: "Schmitt trigger; element with hysteresis" (general qualifying symbols table).
- **HV**: not in the standard. It is our own label for a high-voltage open-collector output.

The standard defines these marks for its rectangle-shaped symbols. Putting them inside the curved (distinctive-shape) gates, as these drawings do, is our choice; the standard does not cover it.

## Parts

### 7400 - Quadruple 2-input NAND gate

- **Part:** 7400
- **Description:** Quadruple 2-input NAND gate
- **Formula:** Y = NOT(A AND B)
- **Manual page:** 4 (PDF page 10)

![7400 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7400.png)

| ND 1976 | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![nand2 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/nand2.png) | ![nand2 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/nand2.png) |

- **Output:** totem-pole
- **What it looks like:** A plain tall rectangle, empty inside. Inputs enter on the left, one output line leaves the middle of the right side.
- **How to tell it apart:** Empty box = NAND. No X, no diagonal, no triangle, no second box.

- **Datasheet:** [TI SN5400/SN7400/SN74LS00/SN74S00 datasheet SDLS025D](https://www.ti.com/lit/ds/symlink/sn7400.pdf) p. 3-4, 7 (figures for SN7400)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = NOT(1A AND 1B)` - 1Y is the NAND of 1A, 1B.
  - `2Y = NOT(2A AND 2B)` - 2Y is the NAND of 2A, 2B.
  - `3Y = NOT(3B AND 3A)` - 3Y is the NAND of 3B, 3A.
  - `4Y = NOT(4B AND 4A)` - 4Y is the NAND of 4B, 4A.

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1A | input | - |
| 2 | 1B | input | - |
| 3 | 1Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 4 | 2A | input | - |
| 5 | 2B | input | - |
| 6 | 2Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | 3Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 9 | 3B | input | - |
| 10 | 3A | input | - |
| 11 | 4Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | 4B | input | - |
| 13 | 4A | input | - |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| any | Y | low to high | - | 11 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 400 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| any | Y | high to low | - | 7 | 15 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 400 ohm, CL 15 pF |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high - mA
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** outputHighMaxCurrentMa: the recommended-operating-conditions row for IOH -0.4 mA names 'SN5400, SN54LS00, and SN74LS00' and does not name SN7400 (the VOH test condition does use IOH -0.4 mA).
- **Notes:** The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delay; minNs is null (the path-delay function uses 0 ns). No setup/hold/pulse-width limits apply to a gate, so timingLimits is empty. The pin table gives 3A=10, 3B=9, 4A=13, 4B=12 (A and B are interchangeable in a NAND).

### 7401 - Quadruple 2-input NAND gate with open collector output

- **Part:** 7401
- **Description:** Quadruple 2-input NAND gate with open collector output
- **Formula:** Y = NOT(A AND B)
- **Manual page:** 4 (PDF page 10)

![7401 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7401.png)

| ND 1976 | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![nand-oc2 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/nand-oc2.png) | ![nand-oc2 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/nand-oc2.png) |

- **Output:** open-collector
- **What it looks like:** The NAND rectangle with one vertical line down the centre of the box, dividing it into two halves.
- **How to tell it apart:** Centre vertical bar = open collector. Without the bar it is the plain NAND (7400).

- **Datasheet:** [TI SN5401/SN54LS01/SN7401/SN74LS01 datasheet SDLS026](https://www.ti.com/lit/ds/symlink/sn5401.pdf) p. 1, 3 (figures for SN7401)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = NOT(1A AND 1B)` - 1Y is the NAND of 1A, 1B. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `2Y = NOT(2A AND 2B)` - 2Y is the NAND of 2A, 2B. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `3Y = NOT(3A AND 3B)` - 3Y is the NAND of 3A, 3B. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `4Y = NOT(4A AND 4B)` - 4Y is the NAND of 4A, 4B. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.

| 6 | 2B | input | - |

- **Electrical notes:** Open collector: no IOH figure applies; the datasheet gives an off-state leakage of max 0.25 mA at VOH 5.5 V instead.
- **Notes:** The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delay; minNs is null (the path-delay function uses 0 ns). No setup/hold/pulse-width limits apply to a gate, so timingLimits is empty. Pinout differs from 7400/7403: outputs on pins 1, 4, 10, 13. tPLH max read from a poor scan as 55 ns.

### 7406 - Hex inverter buffer/driver with open-collector high-voltage output

- **Part:** 7406
- **Description:** Hex inverter buffer/driver with open-collector high-voltage output
- **Formula:** Y = NOT A
- **Manual page:** 5 (PDF page 11)

![7406 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7406.png)

| ND 1976 | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![not-buf-ochv1 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/not-buf-ochv1.png) | ![not-buf-ochv1 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/not-buf-ochv1.png) |

- **Output:** open-collector-high-voltage
- **What it looks like:** A box whose right side is drawn as a point, with one diagonal from the bottom left corner to the tip of the point. High-voltage open collector: works like a plain open collector (it can only pull the line low; a pull-up resistor makes the 1, and several outputs may share one line), but the output transistor stands a higher voltage and a larger current. Per the TI datasheets: 7406/7407 up to 30 V, 7416/7417 up to 15 V, and up to 40 mA pulled low; a plain open collector such as 7405 stands only 5.5 V and 16 mA, so its pull-up must go to +5 V. High-voltage parts can switch a lamp, a relay or a 12 V line directly. The logic is the same. In the modern drawing it is marked HV beside the open-collector mark; that label is ours, not a standard symbol.
- **How to tell it apart:** Pointed right end = buffer/driver. Diagonal inside = open-collector, inverting.

- **Datasheet:** [TI SN5406/SN5416/SN7406/SN7416 datasheet SDLS031A](https://www.ti.com/lit/ds/symlink/sn7406.pdf) p. 1, 3 (figures for SN7406)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = NOT 1A` - 1Y is the inverse of 1A. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `2Y = NOT 2A` - 2Y is the inverse of 2A. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `3Y = NOT 3A` - 3Y is the inverse of 3A. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `4Y = NOT 4A` - 4Y is the inverse of 4A. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `5Y = NOT 5A` - 5Y is the inverse of 5A. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `6Y = NOT 6A` - 6Y is the inverse of 6A. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1A | input | - |
| 2 | 1Y | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage |
| 3 | 2A | input | - |
| 4 | 2Y | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage |
| 5 | 3A | input | - |
| 6 | 3Y | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | 4Y | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage |
| 9 | 4A | input | - |
| 10 | 5Y | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage |
| 11 | 5A | input | - |
| 12 | 6Y | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage |
| 13 | 6A | input | - |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| any | Y | low to high | - | 10 | 15 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 110 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| any | Y | high to low | - | 15 | 23 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 110 ohm, CL 15 pF |

- **Output current:** low 40 mA, high - mA; output may be pulled up to 30 V
- **Electrical notes:** Buffer/driver: IOL 40 mA vs 16 mA for 7404, so it sinks two and a half times as much (lamps, relays, long lines); the output may be pulled up to 30 V (7416: 15 V). Off-state leakage max 0.25 mA at VOH 30 V.
- **Notes:** The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delay; minNs is null (the path-delay function uses 0 ns). No setup/hold/pulse-width limits apply to a gate, so timingLimits is empty.

- **What it looks like:** One rectangle split down the middle: left half empty, right half holds one diagonal from its bottom left corner up to the middle of the right edge.

  - `3Y = 3A AND 3B` - 3Y is the AND of 3A, 3B. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `4Y = 4A AND 4B` - 4Y is the AND of 4A, 4B. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.

### 7413 - Dual 4-input NAND Schmitt trigger

- **Part:** 7413
- **Description:** Dual 4-input NAND Schmitt trigger
- **Formula:** Y = NOT(A AND B AND C AND D)
- **Manual page:** 7 (PDF page 13)

![7413 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7413.png)

| ND 1976 | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![nand-schmitt4 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/nand-schmitt4.png) | ![nand-schmitt4 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/nand-schmitt4.png) |

- **Output:** totem-pole
- **What it looks like:** The empty NAND rectangle with four inputs; its top edge runs on to the right and its bottom edge runs on to the left. What a Schmitt trigger is: a normal TTL input switches at one voltage, so a slow or noisy signal near that voltage makes the output flicker between 0 and 1 several times. A Schmitt trigger input has two switching points instead: the input must rise above about 1.7 V before the output changes, and then fall below about 0.9 V before it changes back. The gap between them (the hysteresis) means noise and slow edges give one clean change. It is used on signals from cables, switches and RC delay circuits. The input still reads as plain logic 0 or 1; only its noise tolerance differs.
- **How to tell it apart:** Edges running past the box (top to the right, bottom to the left) = Schmitt trigger input.

- **Datasheet:** [NTE Electronics NTE7413 datasheet (TTL dual 4-input positive NAND Schmitt trigger, 7413 replacement)](https://cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0268/7746/0673/files/107413.pdf) p. 1-3 (figures for NTE7413)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = NOT(1A AND 1B AND 1C AND 1D)` - 1Y is the NAND of 1A, 1B, 1C, 1D.
  - `2Y = NOT(2A AND 2B AND 2C AND 2D)` - 2Y is the NAND of 2A, 2B, 2C, 2D.

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1A | input | Schmitt input |
| 2 | 1B | input | Schmitt input |
| 3 | NC | nc | - |
| 4 | 1C | input | Schmitt input |
| 5 | 1D | input | Schmitt input |
| 6 | 1Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | 2Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 9 | 2A | input | Schmitt input |
| 10 | 2B | input | Schmitt input |
| 11 | NC | nc | - |
| 12 | 2C | input | Schmitt input |
| 13 | 2D | input | Schmitt input |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| any | Y | low to high | - | 18 | 27 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 400 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| any | Y | high to low | - | 15 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 400 ohm, CL 15 pF |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Schmitt inputs at VCC 5 V: VT+ min 1.5 / typ 1.7 / max 2.0 V; VT- min 0.6 / typ 0.9 / max 1.1 V; hysteresis min 0.4 / typ 0.8 V.
- **Notes:** The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delay; minNs is null (the path-delay function uses 0 ns). No setup/hold/pulse-width limits apply to a gate, so timingLimits is empty. No TI SN7413 datasheet could be downloaded (the TI links return an error page); the figures are from the NTE7413 replacement-part sheet, not an original TI/National sheet.

- **Description:** Hex Schmitt-trigger inverter

- **Output:** totem-pole
- **What it looks like:** The inverter rectangle (triangle inside) with its top edge running on to the right and its bottom edge running on to the left. What a Schmitt trigger is: a normal TTL input switches at one voltage, so a slow or noisy signal near that voltage makes the output flicker between 0 and 1 several times. A Schmitt trigger input has two switching points instead: the input must rise above about 1.7 V before the output changes, and then fall below about 0.9 V before it changes back. The gap between them (the hysteresis) means noise and slow edges give one clean change. It is used on signals from cables, switches and RC delay circuits. The input still reads as plain logic 0 or 1; only its noise tolerance differs.
- **How to tell it apart:** Inverter + extended edges = Schmitt inverter.

  - `4Y = NOT 4A` - 4Y is the inverse of 4A.
  - `5Y = NOT 5A` - 5Y is the inverse of 5A.
  - `6Y = NOT 6A` - 6Y is the inverse of 6A.

### 7416 - Hex inverter buffer/driver with open-collector high-voltage output

- **Part:** 7416
- **Description:** Hex inverter buffer/driver with open-collector high-voltage output
- **Formula:** Y = NOT A
- **Manual page:** 7 (PDF page 13)

![7416 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7416.png)

| ND 1976 | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![not-buf-ochv-bar1 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/not-buf-ochv-bar1.png) | ![not-buf-ochv-bar1 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/not-buf-ochv-bar1.png) |

- **Output:** open-collector-high-voltage
- **What it looks like:** A box with a pointed right end and one vertical bar about a third of the way in from the left edge. High-voltage open collector: works like a plain open collector (it can only pull the line low; a pull-up resistor makes the 1, and several outputs may share one line), but the output transistor stands a higher voltage and a larger current. Per the TI datasheets: 7406/7407 up to 30 V, 7416/7417 up to 15 V, and up to 40 mA pulled low; a plain open collector such as 7405 stands only 5.5 V and 16 mA, so its pull-up must go to +5 V. High-voltage parts can switch a lamp, a relay or a 12 V line directly. The logic is the same. In the modern drawing it is marked HV beside the open-collector mark; that label is ours, not a standard symbol.
- **How to tell it apart:** Bar inside the pointed box = open collector, inverting driver (high voltage). Compare 7406 (diagonal instead of bar).

- **Datasheet:** [TI SN5406/SN5416/SN7406/SN7416 datasheet SDLS031A](https://www.ti.com/lit/ds/symlink/sn7416.pdf) p. 1, 3 (figures for SN7416)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = NOT 1A` - 1Y is the inverse of 1A. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `2Y = NOT 2A` - 2Y is the inverse of 2A. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `3Y = NOT 3A` - 3Y is the inverse of 3A. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `4Y = NOT 4A` - 4Y is the inverse of 4A. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `5Y = NOT 5A` - 5Y is the inverse of 5A. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `6Y = NOT 6A` - 6Y is the inverse of 6A. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1A | input | - |
| 2 | 1Y | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage |
| 3 | 2A | input | - |
| 4 | 2Y | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage |
| 5 | 3A | input | - |
| 6 | 3Y | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | 4Y | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage |
| 9 | 4A | input | - |
| 10 | 5Y | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage |
| 11 | 5A | input | - |
| 12 | 6Y | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage |
| 13 | 6A | input | - |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| any | Y | low to high | - | 10 | 15 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 110 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| any | Y | high to low | - | 15 | 23 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 110 ohm, CL 15 pF |

- **Output current:** low 40 mA, high - mA; output may be pulled up to 15 V
- **Electrical notes:** Buffer/driver: IOL 40 mA vs 16 mA for 7404, so it sinks two and a half times as much; the output may be pulled up to 15 V (7406: 30 V). Off-state leakage max 0.25 mA at VOH 15 V.
- **Notes:** The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delay; minNs is null (the path-delay function uses 0 ns). No setup/hold/pulse-width limits apply to a gate, so timingLimits is empty.

- **Output current:** low - mA, high - mA
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** No 74H21 (high-speed TTL) datasheet was found. Pins and function come from the SN74LS21 sheet (same pinout); outputLowMaxCurrentMa, outputHighMaxCurrentMa and the real 74H propagation delays are unknown.
- **Notes:** The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delay; minNs is null (the path-delay function uses 0 ns). No setup/hold/pulse-width limits apply to a gate, so timingLimits is empty. The timing entries are SN74LS figures (LS family) and must not be read as 74H values.

### 7425 - Dual 4-input NOR gate with strobe

- **Part:** 7425
- **Description:** Dual 4-input NOR gate with strobe
- **Formula:** Y = NOT(G AND (A OR B OR C OR D))
- **Manual page:** 9 (PDF page 15)

![7425 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7425.png)

| ND 1976 | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![nor-strobe4 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/nor-strobe4.png) | ![nor-strobe4 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/nor-strobe4.png) |

- **Output:** totem-pole
- **What it looks like:** A tall X box with four inputs, and a fifth input G entering from below the box (strobe).
- **How to tell it apart:** X box with an input from underneath = NOR with strobe.

- **Datasheet:** [TI SN5423/SN5425/SN7423/SN7425 datasheet SDLS082](https://www.ti.com/lit/ds/symlink/sn7425.pdf) p. 1-3 (figures for SN7425)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = NOT(1G AND (1A OR 1B OR 1C OR 1D))` - When 1G is high, 1Y is low if any of 1A, 1B, 1C, 1D is high, else high; when 1G is low, 1Y is high.
  - `2Y = NOT(2G AND (2A OR 2B OR 2C OR 2D))` - When 2G is high, 2Y is low if any of 2A, 2B, 2C, 2D is high, else high; when 2G is low, 2Y is high.

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1A | input | - |
| 2 | 1B | input | - |
| 3 | 1G | enable | active: high; whenInactive: high; controls: 6 |
| 4 | 1C | input | - |
| 5 | 1D | input | - |
| 6 | 1Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | 2Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 9 | 2A | input | - |
| 10 | 2B | input | - |
| 11 | 2G | enable | active: high; whenInactive: high; controls: 8 |
| 12 | 2C | input | - |
| 13 | 2D | input | - |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| any | Y | low to high | - | 13 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 400 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| any | Y | high to low | - | 8 | 15 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 400 ohm, CL 15 pF |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Strobe inputs load more than data inputs: IIH 160 uA vs 40 uA, IIL -6.4 mA vs -1.6 mA.
- **Notes:** The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delay; minNs is null (the path-delay function uses 0 ns). No setup/hold/pulse-width limits apply to a gate, so timingLimits is empty. The switching table covers the '23 and '25 together, measured with fan-out N = 10.

### 7428 - Quadruple 2-input NOR buffer

- **Description:** Quadruple 2-input NOR buffer
- **Formula:** Y = NOT(A OR B)

- **Output:** buffer
- **What it looks like:** A rectangle with an X running into all four corners; the rectangle's right edge is followed by a pointed end.
- **How to tell it apart:** X box + point = NOR buffer. Compare 7433, which adds a line down the centre of the box.

- **Datasheet:** [TI SN5428/SN54LS28/SN7428/SN74LS28 datasheet SDLS094](https://www.ti.com/lit/ds/symlink/sn5428.pdf) p. 1, 3 (figures for SN7428)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = NOT(1A OR 1B)` - 1Y is the NOR of 1A, 1B.
  - `2Y = NOT(2A OR 2B)` - 2Y is the NOR of 2A, 2B.
  - `3Y = NOT(3A OR 3B)` - 3Y is the NOR of 3A, 3B.
  - `4Y = NOT(4A OR 4B)` - 4Y is the NOR of 4A, 4B.

| 1 | 1Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |

| 3 | 1B | input | - |

| 6 | 2B | input | - |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | 3A | input | - |
| 9 | 3B | input | - |

| 11 | 4A | input | - |
| 12 | 4B | input | - |
| 13 | 4Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |

| any | Y | low to high | - | 10 | 15 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 133 ohm, CL 150 pF |
| any | Y | high to low | - | 12 | 18 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 133 ohm, CL 150 pF |

- **Output current:** low 48 mA, high -2.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Buffer: IOL 48 mA vs 16 mA for 7402 and IOH -2.4 mA vs -0.4 mA: can drive three times as many inputs or a long line. IOS -70 to -180 mA.
- **Notes:** The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delay; minNs is null (the path-delay function uses 0 ns). No setup/hold/pulse-width limits apply to a gate, so timingLimits is empty.

### 7433 - Quadruple 2-input NOR buffer with open-collector

- **Part:** 7433
- **Description:** Quadruple 2-input NOR buffer with open-collector
- **Formula:** Y = NOT(A OR B)
- **Manual page:** 10 (PDF page 16)

![7433 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7433.png)

| ND 1976 | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![nor-buf-oc2 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/nor-buf-oc2.png) | ![nor-buf-oc2 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/nor-buf-oc2.png) |

- **Output:** open-collector
- **What it looks like:** The 7428 symbol (X box running into all four corners, then a pointed end) with a vertical line down the centre of the box.
- **How to tell it apart:** Centre line in the box = open collector, the same mark as on 7401/7403.

- **Datasheet:** [TI SN5433/SN54LS33/SN7433/SN74LS33 datasheet SDLS101](https://www.ti.com/lit/ds/symlink/sn74ls33.pdf) p. 1, 3 (figures for SN7433)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = NOT(1A OR 1B)` - 1Y is the NOR of 1A, 1B. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `2Y = NOT(2A OR 2B)` - 2Y is the NOR of 2A, 2B. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `3Y = NOT(3A OR 3B)` - 3Y is the NOR of 3A, 3B. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.
  - `4Y = NOT(4A OR 4B)` - 4Y is the NOR of 4A, 4B. Open collector: the high level needs an outside pull-up.

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1Y | output | outputType: open-collector |
| 2 | 1A | input | - |
| 3 | 1B | input | - |
| 4 | 2Y | output | outputType: open-collector |
| 5 | 2A | input | - |
| 6 | 2B | input | - |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | 3A | input | - |
| 9 | 3B | input | - |
| 10 | 3Y | output | outputType: open-collector |
| 11 | 4A | input | - |
| 12 | 4B | input | - |
| 13 | 4Y | output | outputType: open-collector |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| any | Y | low to high | - | 10 | 15 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 133 (the scan prints kohm), CL 50 pF |
| any | Y | high to low | - | 12 | 18 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 133 (the scan prints kohm), CL 50 pF |
| any | Y | low to high | - | 15 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 133 (the scan prints kohm), CL 150 pF |
| any | Y | high to low | - | 16 | 24 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 133 (the scan prints kohm), CL 150 pF |

- **Output current:** low 48 mA, high - mA; output may be pulled up to 5.5 V
- **Electrical notes:** Open-collector buffer: IOL 48 mA vs 16 mA for 7402: can drive three times as many inputs or a long line. Open collector: no IOH figure applies; the datasheet gives an off-state leakage of max 0.25 mA at VOH 5.5 V instead.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Load resistor unit in the switching table: the scan reads '133 kohm'; the sister parts 7428/7437/7438 print 133 ohm. Not settled from the 7433 sheet itself.
- **Notes:** The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delay; minNs is null (the path-delay function uses 0 ns). No setup/hold/pulse-width limits apply to a gate, so timingLimits is empty.

| 12 | 2C | input | - |

| any | Y | high to low | - | 8 | 15 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 133 ohm |

### 7442 - 4-line-to-10-line decoder (1-of-10)

- **Part:** 7442
- **Description:** 4-line-to-10-line decoder (1-of-10)
- **Formula:** 0 = NOT (D' AND C' AND B' AND A'); 1 = NOT (D' AND C' AND B' AND A); 2 = NOT (D' AND C' AND B AND A') ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 11 (PDF page 17)

![7442 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7442.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7442 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7442.png) | ![7442 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7442.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 7 (MSI/LSI data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-15, 7-16, 7-17 (PDF pages 14-16) (figures for SN7442A (the 1976 book lists only the A version of the plain 74 part))
- **Formulas:**
  - `0 = NOT (D' AND C' AND B' AND A')` - Output 0 is low (active) only when DCBA = 0000; inputs 10-15 leave every output high.
  - `1 = NOT (D' AND C' AND B' AND A)` - Output 1 is low (active) only when DCBA = 0001; inputs 10-15 leave every output high.
  - `2 = NOT (D' AND C' AND B AND A')` - Output 2 is low (active) only when DCBA = 0010; inputs 10-15 leave every output high.
  - `3 = NOT (D' AND C' AND B AND A)` - Output 3 is low (active) only when DCBA = 0011; inputs 10-15 leave every output high.
  - `4 = NOT (D' AND C AND B' AND A')` - Output 4 is low (active) only when DCBA = 0100; inputs 10-15 leave every output high.
  - `5 = NOT (D' AND C AND B' AND A)` - Output 5 is low (active) only when DCBA = 0101; inputs 10-15 leave every output high.
  - `6 = NOT (D' AND C AND B AND A')` - Output 6 is low (active) only when DCBA = 0110; inputs 10-15 leave every output high.
  - `7 = NOT (D' AND C AND B AND A)` - Output 7 is low (active) only when DCBA = 0111; inputs 10-15 leave every output high.
  - `8 = NOT (D AND C' AND B' AND A')` - Output 8 is low (active) only when DCBA = 1000; inputs 10-15 leave every output high.
  - `9 = NOT (D AND C' AND B' AND A)` - Output 9 is low (active) only when DCBA = 1001; inputs 10-15 leave every output high.

|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 0 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 2 | 1 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 3 | 2 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 4 | 3 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 5 | 4 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 6 | 5 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | 6 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | 7 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | 8 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | 9 | output | outputType: totem-pole |

| 13 | C | input | - |
| 14 | B | input | - |
| 15 | A | input | - |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A,B,C,D | any | high to low | - | 14 | 25 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, through 2 levels of logic |
| A,B,C,D | any | high to low | - | 17 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, through 3 levels of logic |
| A,B,C,D | any | low to high | - | 10 | 25 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, through 2 levels of logic |
| A,B,C,D | any | low to high | - | 17 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, through 3 levels of logic |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** The SN7442A column of the recommended operating conditions prints IOH as '800' uA without the minus sign; the SN5442A column and the VOH test condition (IOH = -800 uA) show it is -800 uA out of the pin.
- **Notes:** Datasheet does not say which output paths go through 2 and which through 3 levels of logic; timing gives both rows for all outputs. The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delays; the path-delay function uses 0 ns for the minimum.

- **Output current:** low 80 mA, high - mA; output may be pulled up to 30 V
- **Electrical notes:** Open-collector, 30 V off-state output voltage (recommended operating conditions). Sinks up to 80 mA per output (description; VO(on) max 0.9 V at 80 mA, 0.4 V at 20 mA). Off-state leakage max 250 uA at 30 V. Lamp, relay or MOS driver.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Typical propagation delays: the table gives maximum only.; IOL is not in the recommended-operating-conditions table; 80 mA is the figure from the description and the VO(on) test condition.; IOH as a source current does not apply (open collector); outputHighMaxCurrentMa is null.
- **Notes:** The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delays; the path-delay function uses 0 ns for the minimum.

### 7450 - 2x2 AND-OR-INVERT (7450 - 7451 - 7460)

- **Part:** 7450
- **Description:** 2x2 AND-OR-INVERT (7450 - 7451 - 7460)
- **Formula:** 2x2 AND-OR-INVERT
- **Manual page:** 12 (PDF page 18)

![7450 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7450.png)

| ND 1976 | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![aoi-2x28 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/aoi-2x28.png) | ![aoi-2x28 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/aoi-2x28.png) |

- **Output:** totem-pole
- **What it looks like:** Boxes stacked on the left (the AND parts) feeding an X box (the OR-invert). The manual notes the four-input gates are 7460 circuits (expanders). 7451 is drawn the same way.
- **How to tell it apart:** Stacked boxes feeding an X box = AND-OR-INVERT.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 5 (pin assignments and function tables)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/05.pdf) p. 5-16 (PDF page 16) (figures for SN7450 (J, N package)); [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 6 (gate and flip-flop data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/06.pdf) p. 6-39, 6-40, 6-41 (PDF pages 39-41) (figures for SN7450)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = NOT ((1A AND 1B) OR (1C AND 1D) OR X)` - Gate 1 (expandable): 1Y is low when 1A and 1B are both high, or 1C and 1D are both high, or an expander on X/X' has all its inputs high. X = output of a 7460 expander.
  - `2Y = NOT ((2A AND 2B) OR (2C AND 2D))` - Gate 2: 2Y is low when 2A and 2B are both high, or 2C and 2D are both high.

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1A | input | - |
| 2 | 2A | input | - |
| 3 | 2B | input | - |
| 4 | 2C | input | - |
| 5 | 2D | input | - |
| 6 | 2Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | 1Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 9 | 1C | input | - |
| 10 | 1D | input | - |
| 11 | X | analog | text: expander node of gate 1: connect to the X output of a 7460 expander; leave open if not used |
| 12 | X' | analog | text: expander node of gate 1: connect to the X-bar output of a 7460 expander; leave open if not used |
| 13 | 1B | input | - |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| any | Y | low to high | - | 13 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, expander pins open |
| any | Y | high to low | - | 8 | 15 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, expander pins open |
| 7460 expander input | 1Y | low to high | - | 15 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, from an input of a 7460 expander |
| 7460 expander input | 1Y | high to low | - | 10 | 20 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, from an input of a 7460 expander |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Designed for use with up to four 7460 expanders. Expander current IX' max -3.1 mA (SN7450, VCC MIN, TA MIN).
- **Notes:** The manual title groups 7450, 7451 and 7460; this file describes the 7450 (one gate expandable, one not). The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delays; the path-delay function uses 0 ns for the minimum.

| any | Y | low to high | - | 7 | 11 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 25 pF, RL 280 ohm, expander pins open |
| any | Y | high to low | - | 6.5 | 11 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 25 pF, RL 280 ohm, expander pins open |
| any | Y | low to high | - | 11.4 | - | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 25 pF, RL 280 ohm, C = 15 pF from GND to X' |
| any | Y | high to low | - | 7.7 | - | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 25 pF, RL 280 ohm, C = 15 pF from GND to X' |

- **Output current:** low 20 mA, high -0.5 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Designed for use with up to four 74H60 expanders or one 74H62 expander.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Maximum propagation delay with 15 pF on the expander node: the table gives typical values only.
- **Notes:** The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delays; the path-delay function uses 0 ns for the minimum.

### 7470 - J-K flip-flop

- **Part:** 7470
- **Description:** J-K flip-flop
- **Formula:** Q = state; Q' = NOT state (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 15 (PDF page 21)

![7470 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7470.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7470 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7470.png) | ![7470 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7470.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 5 (pin assignments and function tables)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/05.pdf) p. 5-21 (PDF page 21) (figures for SN7470 (J, N package)); [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 6 (gate and flip-flop data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/06.pdf) p. 6-46, 6-47, 6-48 (PDF pages 46-48) (figures for SN7470)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Q = state` - Q is the stored bit (low while PR and CLR are both low).
  - `Q' = NOT state` - Q' is the inverse of the stored bit (low while PR and CLR are both low).
- **Stored state:** 1 bits, changes on rising edge of CK
  - J = J1 AND J2 AND NOT J'; K = K1 AND K2 AND NOT K'
  - PR low, CLR high, CK low: Q = 1, Q' = 0 at once
  - PR high, CLR low, CK low: Q = 0, Q' = 1 at once
  - PR low, CLR low (CK don't care): Q = 0 and Q' = 0 (table prints L* L*); not stable, does not persist when PR and CLR return high
  - Preset or clear can occur only when CK is low
  - PR high, CLR high, rising edge of CK: J=0 K=0 hold; J=1 K=0 Q=1; J=0 K=1 Q=0; J=1 K=1 toggle
  - PR high, CLR high, CK low: hold

|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | NC | nc | - |

| 4 | J2 | input | - |

| 6 | Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 9 | K' | input | text: inverted K input; must be grounded if not used |
| 10 | K1 | input | - |
| 11 | K2 | input | - |

| 13 | PR | input | active: low; controls: 8, 6; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| PR | Q' | high to low | - | - | 50 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLR | Q' | low to high | - | - | 50 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLR | Q | high to low | - | - | 50 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.4 mA
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Typical preset and clear propagation delays: the table gives the maximum (50 ns) only.
- **Notes:** The 7470 function table prints PR=L, CLR=L as Q = L, Q' = L (marked nonstable); the 7472/7474/7476 tables in the same book print H H for that case. The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delays; the path-delay function uses 0 ns for the minimum.

### 7472 - J-K master-slave flip-flop

- **Part:** 7472
- **Description:** J-K master-slave flip-flop
- **Formula:** Q = state; Q' = NOT state (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 16 (PDF page 22)

![7472 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7472.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7472 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7472.png) | ![7472 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7472.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 5 (pin assignments and function tables)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/05.pdf) p. 5-22 (PDF page 22) (figures for SN7472 (J, N package)); [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 6 (gate and flip-flop data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/06.pdf) p. 6-46, 6-47, 6-48 (PDF pages 46-48) (figures for SN7472)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Q = state` - Q is the stored bit.
  - `Q' = NOT state` - Q' is the inverse of the stored bit (both high while PR and CLR are both low).
- **Stored state:** 1 bits, changes on falling edge of CK (master-slave)
  - J = J1 AND J2 AND J3; K = K1 AND K2 AND K3
  - PR low, CLR high: Q = 1, Q' = 0 at once
  - PR high, CLR low: Q = 0, Q' = 1 at once
  - PR low, CLR low: Q = 1 and Q' = 1; not stable, does not persist when PR and CLR return high
  - Master-slave, pulse triggered: J and K are taken in while CK is high (setup 0 ns before the rising edge, hold 0 ns after the falling edge); the outputs change on the falling edge of CK
  - On a CK pulse with clear/preset inactive: J=0 K=0 hold; J=1 K=0 Q=1; J=0 K=1 Q=0; J=1 K=1 toggle

|---|---|---|---|

| 11 | K3 | input | - |

### 7475 - Quadruple bistable latch

- **Part:** 7475
- **Description:** Quadruple bistable latch
- **Formula:** 1Q = state1; 1Q' = NOT state1; 2Q = state2 ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 17 (PDF page 23)

![7475 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7475.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7475 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7475.png) | ![7475 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7475.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 5 (pin assignments and function tables)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/05.pdf) p. 5-23 (PDF page 23) (figures for SN7475 (J, N package)); [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 7 (MSI/LSI data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-34 to 7-37 (PDF pages 34-36) (figures for SN7475)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Q = state1` - Latch 1 bit.
  - `1Q' = NOT state1` - Inverse of 1Q.
  - `2Q = state2` - Latch 2 bit.
  - `2Q' = NOT state2` - Inverse of 2Q.
  - `3Q = state3` - Latch 3 bit.
  - `3Q' = NOT state3` - Inverse of 3Q.
  - `4Q = state4` - Latch 4 bit.
  - `4Q' = NOT state4` - Inverse of 4Q.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on level of G12 (latches 1, 2) and G34 (latches 3, 4)
  - G high: Q = D, Q' = NOT D (transparent)
  - G low: Q holds the level it had before the high-to-low transition of G

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |

### 7476 - Dual J-K flip-flop with preset and clear

- **Part:** 7476
- **Description:** Dual J-K flip-flop with preset and clear
- **Formula:** 1Q = state1; 1Q' = NOT state1; 2Q = state2 ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 18 (PDF page 24)

![7476 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7476.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7476 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7476.png) | ![7476 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7476.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 5 (pin assignments and function tables)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/05.pdf) p. 5-23 (PDF page 23) (figures for SN7476 (J, N package)); [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 6 (gate and flip-flop data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/06.pdf) p. 6-46, 6-47, 6-48 (PDF pages 46-48) (figures for SN7476)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Q = state1` - Stored bit of flip-flop 1.
  - `1Q' = NOT state1` - Inverse of 1Q (both high while 1PR and 1CLR are both low).
  - `2Q = state2` - Stored bit of flip-flop 2.
  - `2Q' = NOT state2` - Inverse of 2Q (both high while 2PR and 2CLR are both low).
- **Stored state:** 2 bits, changes on falling edge of nCK (master-slave), each flip-flop on its own clock
  - PR low, CLR high: Q = 1, Q' = 0 at once
  - PR high, CLR low: Q = 0, Q' = 1 at once
  - PR low, CLR low: Q = 1 and Q' = 1; not stable, does not persist when PR and CLR return high
  - Master-slave, pulse triggered: J and K are taken in while CK is high (setup 0 ns before the rising edge, hold 0 ns after the falling edge); the outputs change on the falling edge of CK
  - On a CK pulse with clear/preset inactive: J=0 K=0 hold; J=1 K=0 Q=1; J=0 K=1 Q=0; J=1 K=1 toggle

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1CK | clock | edge: falling |
| 2 | 1PR | input | active: low; controls: 15, 14; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 3 | 1CLR | input | active: low; controls: 15, 14; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 4 | 1J | input | - |
| 5 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |
| 6 | 2CK | clock | edge: falling |
| 7 | 2PR | input | active: low; controls: 11, 10; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 8 | 2CLR | input | active: low; controls: 11, 10; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 9 | 2J | input | - |
| 10 | 2Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | 2Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | 2K | input | - |
| 13 | GND | ground | - |
| 14 | 1Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 15 | 1Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 16 | 1K | input | - |

### 7483 - 4-bit binary full-adder

- **Part:** 7483
- **Description:** 4-bit binary full-adder
- **Formula:** S1 = A1 XOR B1 XOR C0; S2 = A2 XOR B2 XOR C1, C1 = (A1 AND B1) OR ((A1 XOR B1) AND C0); S3 = A3 XOR B3 XOR C2, C2 = (A2 AND B2) OR ((A2 XOR B2) AND C1) ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 18 (PDF page 24)

![7483 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7483.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7483 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7483.png) | ![7483 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7483.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 7 (MSI/LSI data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-52 to 7-55 (PDF pages 52-54) (figures for SN7483A (the 1976 book lists only the A version of the plain 74 part))
- **Formulas:**
  - `S1 = A1 XOR B1 XOR C0` - Sum bit 1 (datasheet name: sigma 1).
  - `S2 = A2 XOR B2 XOR C1, C1 = (A1 AND B1) OR ((A1 XOR B1) AND C0)` - Sum bit 2. C1 is the internal carry out of bit 1.
  - `S3 = A3 XOR B3 XOR C2, C2 = (A2 AND B2) OR ((A2 XOR B2) AND C1)` - Sum bit 3. C2 is the internal carry out of bit 2 (named in the function table note).
  - `S4 = A4 XOR B4 XOR C3, C3 = (A3 AND B3) OR ((A3 XOR B3) AND C2)` - Sum bit 4.
  - `C4 = (A4 AND B4) OR ((A4 XOR B4) AND C3)` - Carry out: C4,S4,S3,S2,S1 = A4..A1 + B4..B1 + C0 in binary. Internally the carry is looked ahead across all four bits (datasheet), so the value is the same as this ripple formula but the delay is not ripple delay.

| 1 | A4 | input | - |
| 2 | S3 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 3 | A3 | input | - |
| 4 | B3 | input | - |
| 5 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |
| 6 | S2 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | B2 | input | - |
| 8 | A2 | input | - |
| 9 | S1 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | A1 | input | - |
| 11 | B1 | input | - |
| 12 | GND | ground | - |
| 13 | C0 | input | - |
| 14 | C4 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 15 | S4 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 16 | B4 | input | - |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C0 | S1,S2,S3,S4 | low to high | - | 14 | 21 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| C0 | S1,S2,S3,S4 | high to low | - | 12 | 21 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| Ai,Bi | Si | low to high | - | 16 | 24 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| Ai,Bi | Si | high to low | - | 16 | 24 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| C0 | C4 | low to high | - | 9 | 14 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 780 ohm |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Sum outputs: IOL 16 mA, IOH -800 uA. Carry output C4: IOL 8 mA, IOH -400 uA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Delay from Ai/Bi to a higher sum bit (for example A1 to S4): the table lists only Ai/Bi to Si (same bit) and to C4.
- **Notes:** Datasheet pin names use the Greek sigma for the sum outputs; written S1-S4 here. The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delays; the path-delay function uses 0 ns for the minimum.

### 7485 - 4-bit comparator

- **Part:** 7485
- **Description:** 4-bit comparator
- **Formula:** A>Bout = (A > B) OR ((A = B) AND A=Bin' AND A<Bin'); A<Bout = (A < B) OR ((A = B) AND A=Bin' AND A>Bin'); A=Bout = (A = B) AND A=Bin (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 19 (PDF page 25)

![7485 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7485.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7485 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7485.png) | ![7485 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7485.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 7 (MSI/LSI data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-56, 7-57, 7-60 (PDF pages 56, 57, 59) (figures for SN7485)
- **Formulas:**
  - `A>Bout = (A > B) OR ((A = B) AND A=Bin' AND A<Bin')` - High when the word A3..A0 is greater than B3..B0. When the words are equal it is high when the A=B and A<B cascade inputs are both low (so A>Bin=H gives H, and all three cascade inputs low gives H as well, per the '85 function table).
  - `A<Bout = (A < B) OR ((A = B) AND A=Bin' AND A>Bin')` - High when A3..A0 is less than B3..B0. When the words are equal it is high when the A=B and A>B cascade inputs are both low.
  - `A=Bout = (A = B) AND A=Bin` - High only when the words are equal and the A=B cascade input is high.

| 4 | A>Bin | input | text: cascade input from a less significant stage |
| 5 | A>Bout | output | outputType: totem-pole |

### 7489 - 64-bit read/write memory

- **Part:** 7489
- **Description:** 64-bit read/write memory
- **Formula:** S1 = NOT (ME' AND WE AND M[D,C,B,A].bit1); S2 = NOT (ME' AND WE AND M[D,C,B,A].bit2); S3 = NOT (ME' AND WE AND M[D,C,B,A].bit3) ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 19 (PDF page 25)

![7489 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7489.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7489 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7489.png) | ![7489 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7489.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor TTL Data Book 1976, DM54/DM7489, L89A 64-Bit Read/Write Memories](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1976_National_TTL_Databook.pdf) p. 2-28, 2-29 (PDF pages 175-176) (figures for DM7489 (National; TI's 1976 TTL book does not carry the 7489))
- **Formulas:**
  - `S1 = NOT (ME' AND WE AND M[D,C,B,A].bit1)` - Sense output 1: while reading (ME low, WE high) it is the complement of bit 1 of the addressed word; while writing (ME low, WE low) and while ME is high it is at logical 1 (open-collector off).
  - `S2 = NOT (ME' AND WE AND M[D,C,B,A].bit2)` - Sense output 2: while reading (ME low, WE high) it is the complement of bit 2 of the addressed word; while writing (ME low, WE low) and while ME is high it is at logical 1 (open-collector off).
  - `S3 = NOT (ME' AND WE AND M[D,C,B,A].bit3)` - Sense output 3: while reading (ME low, WE high) it is the complement of bit 3 of the addressed word; while writing (ME low, WE low) and while ME is high it is at logical 1 (open-collector off).
  - `S4 = NOT (ME' AND WE AND M[D,C,B,A].bit4)` - Sense output 4: while reading (ME low, WE high) it is the complement of bit 4 of the addressed word; while writing (ME low, WE low) and while ME is high it is at logical 1 (open-collector off).
- **Stored state:** 64 bits, changes on while ME and WE are both low (write)
  - 16 words of 4 bits, selected by the four select inputs A, B, C, D (fully decoded)
  - ME low, WE low: write D1-D4 into the addressed word; outputs at logical 1
  - ME low, WE high: read; outputs show the complement of the addressed word
  - ME high: hold (no write); outputs at logical 1
  - Read-out is nondestructive

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | A | input | - |
| 2 | ME | enable | active: low; whenInactive: high; text: MEMORY ENABLE: low selects the chip; high = hold, outputs at logical 1 (off); controls: 5, 7, 9, 11 |
| 3 | WE | input | active: low; text: WRITE ENABLE: low together with ME low writes D1-D4 into the addressed word; high reads |
| 4 | D1 | input | - |
| 5 | S1 | output | outputType: open-collector; enabledBy: 2 |
| 6 | D2 | input | - |
| 7 | S2 | output | outputType: open-collector; enabledBy: 2 |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | S3 | output | outputType: open-collector; enabledBy: 2 |
| 10 | D3 | input | - |
| 11 | S4 | output | outputType: open-collector; enabledBy: 2 |
| 12 | D4 | input | - |
| 13 | D | input | - |
| 14 | C | input | - |
| 15 | B | input | - |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ME | S1-S4 | low to high | - | 23 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 30 pF, RL1 300 ohm, RL2 600 ohm |
| ME | S1-S4 | high to low | - | 23 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 30 pF, RL1 300 ohm, RL2 600 ohm |
| A,B,C,D | S1-S4 | low to high | - | 34 | 50 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 30 pF, RL1 300 ohm, RL2 600 ohm |
| A,B,C,D | S1-S4 | high to low | - | 35 | 50 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 30 pF, RL1 300 ohm, RL2 600 ohm |
| WE (sense recovery after writing) | S1-S4 | low to high | - | 35 | 50 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 30 pF, RL1 300 ohm, RL2 600 ohm; tSR: sense recovery time after writing (edge not stated) |

- **Timing limits:** pulse width low WE minNs 40; setup D1-D4 to WE minNs 0; setup A,B,C,D to WE minNs 0; hold D1-D4 to WE minNs 0; hold A,B,C,D to end of write minNs 5
- **Output current:** low 12 mA, high - mA; output may be pulled up to 5.5 V
- **Electrical notes:** Open-collector outputs (wire-OR). IOL 12 mA, VOL max 0.4 V (DM74). Off-state output current IOH max 20 uA at VOH 5.5 V (DM74). Highest output voltage given: VOH 5.5 V. National lists these under electrical characteristics; the sheet has no separate recommended-operating-conditions table.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Which select input is the least significant address bit: the connection diagram names them A, B, C, D and the logic diagram A0-A3, but the sheet does not tie the two sets of names together.; Reference edge of the setup and hold times with respect to Write Enable: not stated.; Power-up content of the memory: not stated.; IOH as a source current does not apply (open collector); outputHighMaxCurrentMa is null.
- **Notes:** ND symbol title says 64-bit read/write memory; this file uses the National DM7489 sheet, which describes the same 16 x 4 part with open-collector inverted outputs. The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delays; the path-delay function uses 0 ns for the minimum.

### 7490 - Decade counter

- **Part:** 7490
- **Description:** Decade counter
- **Formula:** QA = stateA; QB = stateB; QC = stateC ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 20 (PDF page 26)

![7490 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7490.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7490 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7490.png) | ![7490 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7490.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 7 (MSI/LSI data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-71 to 7-76 (PDF pages 71-75) (figures for SN7490A (the 1976 book lists only the A version of the plain 74 part))
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA = stateA` - Divide-by-two stage, clocked by input A.
  - `QB = stateB` - Bit B of the second section, clocked by input B.
  - `QC = stateC` - Bit C of the second section.
  - `QD = stateD` - Bit D of the second section.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on falling edge of A (QA) and of B (QB, QC, QD)
  - R0(1) and R0(2) both high, and R9(1) or R9(2) low: QD QC QB QA = 0000 at once
  - R9(1) and R9(2) both high: QD QC QB QA = 1001 at once (takes priority over R0)
  - Otherwise (at least one R0 low and at least one R9 low): count
  - QA toggles on each falling edge of A
  - QD QC QB count 0,1,2,3,4 (divide by five) on falling edges of B
  - With QA connected to B and pulses on A: QD QC QB QA count BCD 0-9
  - With QD connected to A and pulses on B: bi-quinary count, QA gives a divide-by-ten square wave

| 3 | R0(2) | input | active: high; controls: 12, 9, 8, 11; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 4 | NC | nc | - |
| 5 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |
| 6 | R9(1) | input | active: high; controls: 12, 9, 8, 11; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |

| 8 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 9 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | GND | ground | - |
| 11 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | NC | nc | - |
| 14 | A | clock | edge: falling; text: clock of the divide-by-two section |

- **Timing limits:** pulse width A minNs 15; pulse width B minNs 30; pulse width R0(1),R0(2) minNs 15; pulse width R9(1),R9(2) minNs 15; setup (reset inactive-state) R0 to A,B minNs 25; max clock frequency A minMHz 32 typMHz 42; max clock frequency B minMHz 16
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Notes:** Edge: the block diagram draws master-slave flip-flops with an inverted clock on A and B; the outputs change on the high-to-low transition. QA is tested at IOL 16 mA plus the B-input load, so QA can drive B and still keep full fan-out. The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delays; the path-delay function uses 0 ns for the minimum.

### 7494 - 4-bit shift register

- **Part:** 7494
- **Description:** 4-bit shift register
- **Formula:** QD = stateD (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 21 (PDF page 27)

![7494 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7494.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7494 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7494.png) | ![7494 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7494.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 7 (MSI/LSI data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-86, 7-87, 7-88 (PDF pages 85-87) (figures for SN7494)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QD = stateD` - Serial output: the last of the four stages A, B, C, D.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - Internal preset of stage X is active when (PE1 AND P1X) OR (PE2 AND P2X); preset and clear do not depend on the clock
  - CLEAR high and all internal presets inactive: QA = QB = QC = QD = 0 at once
  - CLEAR low and the internal preset of a stage active: that stage = 1 at once (all presets active gives 1111)
  - CLEAR low, presets inactive, CLOCK low: hold
  - CLEAR low, presets inactive, rising edge of CLOCK: QA = SER, QB = old QA, QC = old QB, QD = old QC (shift right)
  - Parallel load is done by clearing first and then presetting with PE1 or PE2

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | P1A | input | active: high; text: preset input 1 of stage A, used when PE1 is high; controls: 9; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 2 | P1B | input | active: high; text: preset input 1 of stage B; controls: 9; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 3 | P1C | input | active: high; text: preset input 1 of stage C; controls: 9; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 4 | P1D | input | active: high; text: preset input 1 of stage D; controls: 9; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 5 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |
| 6 | PE1 | input | active: high; text: preset enable 1: while high, every stage whose P1 input is high is set to 1 at once; controls: 9; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 7 | SER | input | - |
| 8 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 9 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole; text: the only output (datasheet: OUTPUT); QA-QC are internal |
| 10 | CLEAR | input | active: high; text: high sets all four stages to 0 at once, while the internal presets are inactive; controls: 9; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 11 | P2D | input | active: high; text: preset input 2 of stage D, used when PE2 is high; controls: 9; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 12 | GND | ground | - |
| 13 | P2C | input | active: high; text: preset input 2 of stage C; controls: 9; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 14 | P2B | input | active: high; text: preset input 2 of stage B; controls: 9; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 15 | PE2 | input | active: high; text: preset enable 2: while high, every stage whose P2 input is high is set to 1 at once; controls: 9; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 16 | P2A | input | active: high; text: preset input 2 of stage A; controls: 9; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |

- **Timing limits:** pulse width CLOCK minNs 35; pulse width CLEAR minNs 30; pulse width preset minNs 30; setup (high-level data) SER to CLOCK rising minNs 35; setup (low-level data) SER to CLOCK rising minNs 25; hold SER to CLOCK rising minNs 0; max clock frequency CLOCK minMHz 10
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.4 mA
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Result when CLEAR is high while an internal preset is active: not in the function table.; Typical delays from preset and clear: the table gives the maximum only.; Setup/hold reference edge is not named in the table; the description says the data must be set up before the rising edge of the clock.
- **Notes:** The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delays; the path-delay function uses 0 ns for the minimum.

### 7495 - 4-bit right-shift, left-shift register

- **Part:** 7495
- **Description:** 4-bit right-shift, left-shift register
- **Formula:** QA = stateA; QB = stateB; QC = stateC ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 21 (PDF page 27)

![7495 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7495.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7495 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7495.png) | ![7495 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7495.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 7 (MSI/LSI data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-89, 7-91 (PDF pages 88, 90) (figures for SN7495A (the 1976 book lists only the A version of the plain 74 part))
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA = stateA` - Register stage A (QA is the stage the serial input enters).
  - `QB = stateB` - Register stage B (QA is the stage the serial input enters).
  - `QC = stateC` - Register stage C (QA is the stage the serial input enters).
  - `QD = stateD` - Register stage D (QA is the stage the serial input enters).
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on falling edge of CK1 (MODE low) or of CK2 (MODE high)
  - MODE high, CK2 high: hold
  - MODE high, falling edge of CK2: QA QB QC QD = A B C D (parallel load; CK1 is ignored, serial entry is inhibited)
  - Shift left = parallel load with QB wired to A, QC to B, QD to C, and serial data on D
  - MODE low, CK1 high: hold
  - MODE low, falling edge of CK1: QA = SER, QB = old QA, QC = old QB, QD = old QC (shift right; CK2 is ignored)
  - Changes on MODE should be made while both clocks are low; the function table also shows hold for MODE rising with CK2 L CK1 L, MODE falling with CK2 L CK1 L, MODE falling with CK2 L CK1 H, MODE rising with CK2 H CK1 L, and MODE rising with CK2 H CK1 H

### 7496 - 5-bit shift register

- **Part:** 7496
- **Description:** 5-bit shift register
- **Formula:** QA = stateA; QB = stateB; QC = stateC ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 22 (PDF page 28)

![7496 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7496.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7496 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7496.png) | ![7496 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7496.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 7 (MSI/LSI data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-95, 7-98 (PDF pages 94, 97) (figures for SN7496)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA = stateA` - Register stage A.
  - `QB = stateB` - Register stage B.
  - `QC = stateC` - Register stage C.
  - `QD = stateD` - Register stage D.
  - `QE = stateE` - Register stage E.
- **Stored state:** 5 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - Clear and preset do not depend on the clock
  - CLEAR low, PE low: QA-QE = 0 at once
  - CLEAR low, all preset inputs A-E low (PE don't care): QA-QE = 0
  - CLEAR high, PE high: each stage whose preset input is high is set to 1 at once; a stage whose preset input is low holds
  - CLEAR high, PE low, CLOCK low: hold
  - CLEAR high, PE low, rising edge of CLOCK: QA = SER, QB = old QA, QC = old QB, QD = old QC, QE = old QD
  - Parallel load: clear first, then apply data on A-E and a high pulse on PE

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 2 | A | input | active: high; text: preset input of stage A: sets it to 1 at once while PE is high; controls: 15; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |

### 74L95 - 4-bit parallel-access shift register

- **Part:** 74L95
- **Description:** 4-bit parallel-access shift register
- **Formula:** QA = stateA; QB = stateB; QC = stateC ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 22 (PDF page 28)

![74L95 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74L95.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74L95 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74L95.png) | ![74L95 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74L95.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 7 (MSI/LSI data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-89, 7-92 (PDF pages 88, 91) (figures for SN74L95 (J, N package))
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA = stateA` - Register stage A (QA is the stage the serial input enters).
  - `QB = stateB` - Register stage B (QA is the stage the serial input enters).
  - `QC = stateC` - Register stage C (QA is the stage the serial input enters).
  - `QD = stateD` - Register stage D (QA is the stage the serial input enters).
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on falling edge of CK1 (MODE low) or of CK2 (MODE high)
  - MODE high, CK2 high: hold
  - MODE high, falling edge of CK2: QA QB QC QD = A B C D (parallel load; CK1 is ignored, serial entry is inhibited)
  - Shift left = parallel load with QB wired to A, QC to B, QD to C, and serial data on D
  - MODE low, CK1 high: hold
  - MODE low, falling edge of CK1: QA = SER, QB = old QA, QC = old QB, QD = old QC (shift right; CK2 is ignored)
  - Changes on MODE should be made while both clocks are low; the function table also shows hold for MODE rising with CK2 L CK1 L, MODE falling with CK2 L CK1 L, MODE falling with CK2 L CK1 H, MODE rising with CK2 H CK1 L, and MODE rising with CK2 H CK1 H

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | SER | input | - |
| 2 | B | input | - |
| 3 | C | input | - |
| 4 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |
| 5 | D | input | - |
| 6 | MODE | input | text: mode control: high = parallel load / shift left on CK2, low = shift right on CK1 |
| 7 | CK1 | clock | edge: falling; text: clock 1, R-shift |
| 8 | CK2 | clock | edge: falling; text: clock 2, L-shift / load |
| 9 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | GND | ground | - |
| 12 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | A | input | - |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CK1,CK2 | QA,QB,QC,QD | low to high | - | 115 | 200 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 50 pF, RL 4 kohm |
| CK1,CK2 | QA,QB,QC,QD | high to low | - | 125 | 200 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 50 pF, RL 4 kohm |

- **Timing limits:** pulse width CK1,CK2 minNs 200; setup (high-level data) SER,A,B,C,D to clock falling minNs 100; setup (low-level data) SER,A,B,C,D to clock falling minNs 120; hold SER,A,B,C,D to clock falling minNs 0; time to enable clock 1 MODE to CK1 minNs 225; time to enable clock 2 MODE to CK2 minNs 200; time to inhibit clock 1 MODE to CK1 minNs 100; time to inhibit clock 2 MODE to CK2 minNs 0; max clock frequency CK1,CK2 minMHz 3 typMHz 5
- **Output current:** low 3.6 mA, high -0.2 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Low-power TTL: IOL 3.6 mA, IOH -200 uA (SN74L95). The 74L95 has a different pinout from the 7495.
- **Notes:** MODE is drawn as an input: it chooses which clock and which data path is used; it does not switch the outputs off. The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delays; the path-delay function uses 0 ns for the minimum.

### 7497 - Synchronous 6-bit binary rate multiplier

- **Part:** 7497
- **Description:** Synchronous 6-bit binary rate multiplier
- **Formula:** Z = NOT( (CLOCK' AND STROBE') AND ( (F AND QA') OR (E AND QA AND QB') OR (D AND QA AND QB AND QC') OR (C AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD') OR (B AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE') OR (A AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE AND QF') ) ); Y = NOT(Z AND UNITY/CASCADE); ENABLE OUTPUT = NOT(ENABLE INPUT' AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE AND QF) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 23 (PDF page 29)

![7497 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7497.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7497 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7497.png) | ![7497 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7497.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN5497, SN7497, bulletin DL-S 7611802 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-102 to 7-105 (PDF pages 101-104) (figures for SN7497)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Z = NOT( (CLOCK' AND STROBE') AND ( (F AND QA') OR (E AND QA AND QB') OR (D AND QA AND QB AND QC') OR (C AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD') OR (B AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE') OR (A AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE AND QF') ) )` - Z is a NOR of six rate gates. Each rate gate is open while CLOCK and STROBE are both low and the 6-bit counter is in the state that belongs to that rate bit; rate input F gives 32 pulses per 64 clocks, E 16, D 8, C 4, B 2, A 1. So with the counter enabled Z gives M low pulses per 64 clock pulses, M = 32F + 16E + 8D + 4C + 2B + A, f_out = M * f_in / 64. With all rate inputs low Z stays high. Gate inputs read from the functional block diagram (see unknown).
  - `Y = NOT(Z AND UNITY/CASCADE)` - Y is the complement of Z while UNITY/CASCADE is high (M high pulses per 64 clocks); a low UNITY/CASCADE forces Y high. Tying UNITY/CASCADE to CLOCK with STROBE high passes the clock inverted to Y.
  - `ENABLE OUTPUT = NOT(ENABLE INPUT' AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE AND QF)` - Goes low once per 64 clocks, when the counter is enabled and reaches all ones (state 63); used as the enable/strobe of the next stage when cascading. Function table gives 1 pulse per 64 clocks. A further input to this gate is drawn in the block diagram but could not be read clearly (see unknown).
- **Stored state:** 6 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR high: counter QA..QF = 0 at once (function table: Y low, Z high, enable output high; note B says the levels of clock and strobe can change Y and Z, and a low unity/cascade keeps Y high)
  - CLEAR low, ENABLE INPUT low: the 6-bit synchronous binary counter (QA least significant) counts up by one on each rising clock edge, 64 states
  - ENABLE INPUT high: the counter holds
  - STROBE high: rate gates inhibited, Z high

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | B | input | text: rate input, weight 2^1 |
| 2 | E | input | text: rate input, weight 2^4 |
| 3 | F | input | text: rate input, weight 2^5 |
| 4 | A | input | text: rate input, weight 2^0 |
| 5 | Z | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 6 | Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | ENABLE OUTPUT | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 10 | STROBE | enable | active: low; whenInactive: high; text: Low enables the rate decoding gates; high inhibits them, so Z stays high (and Y then follows only the unity/cascade input).; controls: 5, 6 |
| 11 | ENABLE INPUT | enable | active: low; whenInactive: hold; text: Low lets the counter count; high holds the counter. Also gates the enable output.; controls: 7 |
| 12 | UNITY/CASCADE | input | text: Second input of the Y gate. Low forces Y high. |
| 13 | CLEAR | input | active: high; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 14 | C | input | text: rate input, weight 2^2 |
| 15 | D | input | text: rate input, weight 2^3 |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ENABLE INPUT | ENABLE OUTPUT | low to high | - | 13 | 20 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| ENABLE INPUT | ENABLE OUTPUT | high to low | - | 14 | 21 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| STROBE | Z | low to high | - | 12 | 18 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| STROBE | Z | high to low | - | 15 | 23 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | Y | low to high | - | 26 | 39 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | Y | high to low | - | 20 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | Z | low to high | - | 12 | 18 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | Z | high to low | - | 17 | 26 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| RATE | Z | low to high | - | 6 | 10 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| RATE | Z | high to low | - | 9 | 14 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| UNITY/CASCADE | Y | low to high | - | 9 | 14 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| UNITY/CASCADE | Y | high to low | - | 6 | 10 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| STROBE | Y | low to high | - | 19 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| STROBE | Y | high to low | - | 22 | 33 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | ENABLE OUTPUT | low to high | - | 19 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

| CLEAR | Z | high to low | - | 15 | 23 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm; the table prints this row under tPHL for the Clear-to-Z path |
| any rate input | Y | low to high | - | 15 | 23 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| any rate input | Y | high to low | - | 15 | 23 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** max clock frequency CLOCK minMHz 25 typMHz 32; pulse width CLOCK minNs 20; pulse width CLEAR minNs 15; setup ENABLE INPUT to CLOCK rising minNs 25; setup ENABLE INPUT to CLOCK falling edge of the previous clock pulse minNs 0 maxNs tw(clock) - 10; hold ENABLE INPUT to CLOCK rising minNs 0 maxNs tw(clock) - 10; hold ENABLE INPUT to CLOCK falling edge of the previous clock pulse minNs 20 maxNs tcp - 10
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Clock frequency 0 to 25 MHz (recommended). Clock input equivalent Req 2 kOhm nominal, other inputs 4 kOhm. Clock input low-level input current -3.2 mA max, other inputs -1.6 mA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Exact gate inputs of the six rate gates and of the enable-output gate were read from a scanned functional block diagram; the gate for rate F reads as using QA', E uses QA and QB', and so on, consistent with the pulse counts in the function table, but the line-by-line wiring is not printed as text.; The enable-output NAND gate has one more input in the block diagram (a line from the QF area) that could not be identified from the scan.
- **Notes:** Pin names follow the datasheet pin-out: B 1, E 2, F 3, A 4, Z 5, Y 6, ENABLE OUTPUT 7, GND 8, CLOCK 9, STROBE 10, ENABLE INPUT 11, UNITY/CASCADE 12, CLEAR 13, C 14, D 15, VCC 16. The recommended-conditions table column header for the 74 part reads 'SN7494' in the book; it is the SN7497 column (misprint in the source).
