### 74120 - Pulse synchronizer

- **Part:** 74120
- **Description:** Pulse synchronizer
- **Formula:** Y = C AND PASS; Y' = NOT Y (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 25 (PDF page 31)

![74120 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74120.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74120 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74120.png) | ![74120 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74120.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54120, SN74120 Dual pulse synchronizers/drivers, bulletin DL-S 7211537 (revised December 1972)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-118 to 7-120 (PDF pages 117-119) (figures for SN74120)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Y = C AND PASS` - While the section is passing pulses, Y carries the clock pulses of C (16 ns typical from C); the start/stop latch and the clock latch decide whether a pulse is passed, and a pulse once started is passed in full (latched, not clipped). Formula is the behaviour described in the text, not the gate-level wiring.
  - `Y' = NOT Y` - Complement of Y (9 ns typical from C).
- **Stored state:** 2 bits, changes on levels and high-to-low transitions on R, S1, S2; clock C
  - R X, S1 L, S2 X: pass output pulses
  - R X, S1 X, S2 L: pass output pulses
  - R L, S1 H, S2 H: inhibit output pulses
  - R H, S1 high-to-low, S2 H: start output pulses
  - R H, S1 H, S2 high-to-low: start output pulses
  - R high-to-low, S1 H, S2 H: stop output pulses
  - R H, S1 H, S2 H: continue (operation started by the last high-to-low transition continues)
  - After start, M low: pulses keep passing until a stop command; M high: only a single clock pulse is passed
  - When stopped by S or R, the setup times decide which is the last pulse. A low-to-high transition on M meeting tsu(H) passes that positive clock pulse, then inhibits remaining pulses. Low-to-high transitions on M in the 20 ns after the falling edge of the clock pulse may or may not let the next pulse through.
  - Once an output pulse has started it is not cut short (C is latch-controlled)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1M | input | text: mode control: low passes a train of pulses, high passes a single pulse. Internal pull-up. |
| 2 | 1S1 | input | text: start/stop latch input (see function table). Internal pull-up. |
| 3 | 1S2 | input | text: start/stop latch input (see function table). Internal pull-up. |
| 4 | 1R | input | text: latch input; a high-to-low transition stops output pulses. Internal pull-up. |
| 5 | 1C | input | text: clock input whose pulses are passed to the outputs; no pull-up |
| 6 | 1Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | 1Y' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | 2Y' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | 2Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | 2C | input | text: clock input, section 2 |
| 12 | 2R | input | text: Internal pull-up. |
| 13 | 2S1 | input | text: Internal pull-up. |
| 14 | 2S2 | input | text: Internal pull-up. |
| 15 | 2M | input | text: mode control, section 2. Internal pull-up. |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C | Y | low to high | - | 14 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 45 pF, RL 133 ohm |
| C | Y | high to low | - | 17 | 25 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 45 pF, RL 133 ohm |
| C | Y' | low to high | - | 10 | 16 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 45 pF, RL 133 ohm |
| C | Y' | high to low | - | 8 | 13 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 45 pF, RL 133 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** setup any input except M to C minNs 12; setup M to C minNs 0; setup M to C minNs 12; hold any input except M to C minNs 3; hold M to C minNs 20
- **Output current:** low 48 mA, high -2.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Clock driver: outputs drive 60 Series 54/74 loads high and 30 loads low. M, R and S inputs have internal pull-up resistors (15 kOhm nominal shown in the input schematic) and may be left open; the C input has none. C input low-level current -3.2 mA max, other inputs -2.1 mA max.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The exact clock edge each setup/hold time refers to is drawn in Figures 2-5 (book page 7-121), which were not read.; The resting levels of Y and Y' while pulses are inhibited are not stated in the text that was read.; Gate-level formula: the functional block diagram was read only as a picture; the formula given is the behaviour from the text and function table.
- **Notes:** Typical power 127 mW per driver.

### 74148 - 8-line-to-3-line priority encoder (equal to 9318)

- **Part:** 74148
- **Description:** 8-line-to-3-line priority encoder (equal to 9318)
- **Formula:** A2 = NOT(EI' AND (4' OR 5' OR 6' OR 7')); A1 = NOT(EI' AND (7' OR 6' OR (5 AND 4 AND 3') OR (5 AND 4 AND 2'))); A0 = NOT(EI' AND (7' OR (6 AND 5') OR (6 AND 4 AND 3') OR (6 AND 4 AND 2 AND 1'))) ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 28 (PDF page 34)

![74148 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74148.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74148 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74148.png) | ![74148 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74148.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54147, SN54148, SN54LS147, SN54LS148, SN74147, SN74148 (TIM9907), SN74LS147, SN74LS148, bulletin DL-S 7611727 (October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-151, 7-153, 7-154 (PDF pages 150, 152, 153) (figures for SN74148)
- **Formulas:**
  - `A2 = NOT(EI' AND (4' OR 5' OR 6' OR 7'))` - Low when enabled and any of inputs 4-7 is low.
  - `A1 = NOT(EI' AND (7' OR 6' OR (5 AND 4 AND 3') OR (5 AND 4 AND 2')))` - Inverted bit 1 of the number of the highest-numbered low input.
  - `A0 = NOT(EI' AND (7' OR (6 AND 5') OR (6 AND 4 AND 3') OR (6 AND 4 AND 2 AND 1')))` - Inverted bit 0 of the number of the highest-numbered low input.
  - `GS = NOT(EI' AND (0' OR 1' OR 2' OR 3' OR 4' OR 5' OR 6' OR 7'))` - Low when enabled and at least one data input is low.
  - `EO = NOT(EI' AND 0 AND 1 AND 2 AND 3 AND 4 AND 5 AND 6 AND 7)` - Low when enabled and no data input is low; feeds EI of the next lower-priority stage.

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 4 | input | text: data input, active low |
| 2 | 5 | input | text: data input, active low |
| 3 | 6 | input | text: data input, active low |
| 4 | 7 | input | text: data input, active low, highest priority |
| 5 | EI | enable | active: low; whenInactive: high; controls: 9, 7, 6, 14, 15 |
| 6 | A2 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | A1 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | A0 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | 0 | input | text: data input, active low, lowest priority |
| 11 | 1 | input | text: data input, active low |
| 12 | 2 | input | text: data input, active low |
| 13 | 3 | input | text: data input, active low |
| 14 | GS | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 15 | EO | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 thru 7 | A0, A1, A2 | low to high | - | 10 | 15 | in-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| 0 thru 7 | A0, A1, A2 | high to low | - | 9 | 14 | in-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| 0 thru 7 | A0, A1, A2 | low to high | - | 13 | 19 | out-of-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| 0 thru 7 | A0, A1, A2 | high to low | - | 12 | 19 | out-of-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| 0 thru 7 | EO | low to high | - | 6 | 10 | out-of-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| 0 thru 7 | EO | high to low | - | 14 | 25 | out-of-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| 0 thru 7 | GS | low to high | - | 18 | 30 | in-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| 0 thru 7 | GS | high to low | - | 14 | 25 | in-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| EI | A0, A1, A2 | low to high | - | 10 | 15 | in-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| EI | A0, A1, A2 | high to low | - | 10 | 15 | in-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| EI | GS | low to high | - | 8 | 12 | in-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| EI | GS | high to low | - | 10 | 15 | in-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| EI | EO | low to high | - | 10 | 15 | in-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| EI | EO | high to low | - | 17 | 30 | in-phase output; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Input 0 is a double load: IIL -3.2 mA max, IIH 80 uA (Req 2 kOhm); other inputs IIL -1.6 mA, IIH 40 uA.
- **Notes:** All data inputs and outputs are active low. Formulas are derived from the '148 function table.

- **Formula:** 1Y = 1G' AND ((B'A' AND 1C0) OR (B'A AND 1C1) OR (BA' AND 1C2) OR (BA AND 1C3)); 2Y = 2G' AND ((B'A' AND 2C0) OR (B'A AND 2C1) OR (BA' AND 2C2) OR (BA AND 2C3)) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 29 (PDF page 35)

SN74153, SN74L153, SN74LS153, SN74S153, bulletin DL-S 7611852 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-165, 7-167 (PDF pages 164, 166) (figures for SN74153)
- **Formulas:**

### 74155 - Dual 2-line-to-4-line decoder/demultiplexer (74156 is equal)

- **Part:** 74155
- **Description:** Dual 2-line-to-4-line decoder/demultiplexer (74156 is equal)
- **Formula:** 1Y0 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND B'A'); 1Y1 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND B'A); 1Y2 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND BA') ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 30 (PDF page 36)

![74155 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74155.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74155 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74155.png) | ![74155 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74155.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54155, SN54156, SN54LS155, SN54LS156, SN74155, SN74156, SN74LS155, SN74LS156, bulletin DL-S 7611850 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-175 to 7-177 (PDF pages 174-176) (figures for SN74155)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y0 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND B'A')` - Low when 1G is low, 1C is high and B,A = 0.
  - `1Y1 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND B'A)` - Low when 1G is low, 1C is high and B,A = 1.
  - `1Y2 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND BA')` - Low when 1G is low, 1C is high and B,A = 2.
  - `1Y3 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND BA)` - Low when 1G is low, 1C is high and B,A = 3.
  - `2Y0 = NOT(2G' AND 2C' AND B'A')` - Low when 2G is low, 2C is low and B,A = 0.
  - `2Y1 = NOT(2G' AND 2C' AND B'A)` - Low when 2G is low, 2C is low and B,A = 1.
  - `2Y2 = NOT(2G' AND 2C' AND BA')` - Low when 2G is low, 2C is low and B,A = 2.
  - `2Y3 = NOT(2G' AND 2C' AND BA)` - Low when 2G is low, 2C is low and B,A = 3.

| 15 | 2C | enable | active: low; whenInactive: high; text: data, section 2; acts as a second active-low strobe; controls: 9, 10, 11, 12 |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| 1C | Y | high to low | - | 20 | 30 | 3 levels of logic; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** 74155 has totem-pole outputs; the 74156 is the open-collector version (its electrical figures were not read).
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** 74156 (open-collector) electrical and switching figures were not read; this file describes the 74155.
- **Notes:** With 1C tied to 2C and 1G tied to 2G it is a 3-line-to-8-line decoder, C (1C/2C) the most significant select: C low selects section 2, C high section 1 (function table).

### 74157 - Quadruple 2-line-to-1-line data selector/multiplexer (74158 has inverted output)

- **Part:** 74157
- **Description:** Quadruple 2-line-to-1-line data selector/multiplexer (74158 has inverted output)
- **Formula:** 1Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 1A) OR (SELECT AND 1B)); 2Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 2A) OR (SELECT AND 2B)); 3Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 3A) OR (SELECT AND 3B)) ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 31 (PDF page 37)

![74157 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74157.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74157 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74157.png) | ![74157 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74157.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54157 ... SN74157, SN74L157, SN74LS157, SN74LS158, SN74S157, SN74S158, bulletin DL-S 7611847 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-181, 7-184 (PDF pages 180, 183) (figures for SN74157)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 1A) OR (SELECT AND 1B))` - 1Y follows 1A when SELECT is low and 1B when SELECT is high; STROBE high forces it low.
  - `2Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 2A) OR (SELECT AND 2B))` - 2Y follows 2A when SELECT is low and 2B when SELECT is high; STROBE high forces it low.
  - `3Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 3A) OR (SELECT AND 3B))` - 3Y follows 3A when SELECT is low and 3B when SELECT is high; STROBE high forces it low.
  - `4Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 4A) OR (SELECT AND 4B))` - 4Y follows 4A when SELECT is low and 4B when SELECT is high; STROBE high forces it low.

### 74161 - Synchronous 4-bit counter

- **Part:** 74161
- **Description:** Synchronous 4-bit counter
- **Formula:** QA,QB,QC,QD = counter state bits 0..3; RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT = ENABLE T AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 31 (PDF page 37)

![74161 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74161.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74161 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74161.png) | ![74161 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74161.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54160 thru SN54163 ... SN74160 thru SN74163 Synchronous 4-bit counters, bulletin DL-S 7611385 (October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-190, 7-196, 7-197 (PDF pages 189, 195, 196) (figures for SN74161)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA,QB,QC,QD = counter state bits 0..3` - QA is the least significant bit.
  - `RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT = ENABLE T AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD` - High at count 15 while ENABLE T is high; the pulse lasts about as long as the high part of QA. Used to enable the next stage.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR low: QA-QD = 0 at once, regardless of clock, load and enable inputs
  - else LOAD low: QA,QB,QC,QD = A,B,C,D at the next rising clock edge, regardless of the enable inputs
  - else ENABLE P and ENABLE T both high: count up by one (binary, 0-15, wraps to 0) at each rising clock edge
  - else (P or T low): hold
  - Low-to-high transitions at LOAD should be avoided while the clock is low if the enable inputs are high at or before the transition
  - High-to-low transitions at ENABLE P or T should occur only while the clock is high

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | CLEAR | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 2 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 3 | A | input | - |
| 4 | B | input | - |
| 5 | C | input | - |
| 6 | D | input | - |
| 7 | ENABLE P | enable | active: high; whenInactive: hold; controls: 14, 13, 12, 11 |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | LOAD | input | active: low; text: synchronous load: acts at the next rising clock edge |
| 10 | ENABLE T | enable | active: high; whenInactive: hold; text: also gates the ripple carry output; when low, the counter holds and RIPPLE CARRY is low; controls: 14, 13, 12, 11, 15 |
| 11 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 15 | RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLOCK | RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT | low to high | - | 23 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT | high to low | - | 23 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | any Q | low to high | - | 13 | 20 | LOAD high; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | any Q | high to low | - | 15 | 23 | LOAD high; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | any Q | low to high | - | 17 | 25 | LOAD low; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | any Q | high to low | - | 19 | 29 | LOAD low; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| ENABLE T | RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT | low to high | - | 11 | 16 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| ENABLE T | RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT | high to low | - | 11 | 16 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLEAR | any Q | high to low | - | 26 | 38 | clear delay measured from the clear input ('161); VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** max clock frequency CLOCK minMHz 25 typMHz 32; pulse width CLOCK minNs 25; pulse width CLEAR minNs 20; setup A, B, C, D to CLOCK minNs 20; setup ENABLE P to CLOCK minNs 20; setup LOAD to CLOCK minNs 25; hold any input to CLOCK minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Clock and ENABLE T inputs are double loads: IIL -3.2 mA, IIH 80 uA; other inputs -1.6 mA, 40 uA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The ripple-carry formula is from the description text (T fed forward, high-level overflow pulse at the maximum count); the logic diagram page was not read gate by gate.
- **Notes:** Binary counter with direct (asynchronous) clear.

  - `QA,QB,QC,QD = counter state bits 0..3` - QA is the least significant bit.

### 74164 - 8-bit shift register

- **Part:** 74164
- **Description:** 8-bit shift register
- **Formula:** QA..QH = register bits 0..7 (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 32 (PDF page 38)

![74164 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74164.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74164 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74164.png) | ![74164 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74164.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54164, SN54L164, SN54LS164, SN74164, SN74L164, SN74LS164 8-bit parallel-out serial shift registers, bulletin DL-S 7611835 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-206, 7-208 (PDF pages 205, 207) (figures for SN74164)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA..QH = register bits 0..7` - Parallel outputs of the 8 stages; QA is the first stage.
- **Stored state:** 8 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR low: QA..QH = 0 at once
  - CLEAR high, CLOCK low: hold
  - CLEAR high, rising CLOCK edge: QA = A AND B; QB..QH = QA..QG before the edge (one-bit shift)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | A | input | text: gated serial input |
| 2 | B | input | text: gated serial input |
| 3 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 4 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 5 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 6 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 9 | CLEAR | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 10 | QE | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | QF | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | QG | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | QH | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLEAR | any Q | high to low | - | 24 | 36 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 800 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| CLEAR | any Q | high to low | - | 28 | 42 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 800 ohm, CL 50 pF |
| CLOCK | any Q | low to high | 8 | 17 | 27 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 800 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| CLOCK | any Q | low to high | 10 | 20 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 800 ohm, CL 50 pF |
| CLOCK | any Q | high to low | 10 | 21 | 32 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 800 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| CLOCK | any Q | high to low | 10 | 25 | 37 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 800 ohm, CL 50 pF |

- **Timing limits:** max clock frequency CLOCK minMHz 25 typMHz 36; pulse width CLOCK or CLEAR minNs 20; setup A, B to CLOCK minNs 15; hold A, B to CLOCK minNs 5
- **Output current:** low 8 mA, high -0.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Note the low output current: IOL 8 mA, IOH -400 uA (half a standard 74 output).
- **Notes:** A low at either serial input makes the next bit shifted in a low; data at the serial inputs may change while the clock is high or low, only data meeting the setup time is taken.

### 74165 - 8-bit shift register

- **Part:** 74165
- **Description:** 8-bit shift register
- **Formula:** QH = register bit H (last stage); QH' = NOT QH (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 33 (PDF page 39)

![74165 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74165.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74165 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74165.png) | ![74165 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74165.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54165, SN54LS165, SN74165, SN74LS165 Parallel-load 8-bit shift registers, bulletin DL-S 7611375 (October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-212, 7-214 (PDF pages 211, 213) (figures for SN74165)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QH = register bit H (last stage)` - Output of the eighth stage.
  - `QH' = NOT QH` - Complement of the eighth stage.
- **Stored state:** 8 bits, changes on rising edge of (CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT) while SHIFT/LOAD is high
  - SHIFT/LOAD low: QA..QH = A..H at once (direct overriding load)
  - SHIFT/LOAD high, CLOCK INHIBIT low, CLOCK low: hold
  - SHIFT/LOAD high, CLOCK INHIBIT low, rising CLOCK edge: QA = SERIAL INPUT, QB..QH = QA..QG before the edge (shift toward QH)
  - SHIFT/LOAD high, CLOCK INHIBIT high: hold
  - The clock is a 2-input NOR of CLOCK and CLOCK INHIBIT; holding either high inhibits clocking, holding either low lets the other be the clock

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | SHIFT/LOAD | input | active: low; text: low loads A..H into the register directly, independent of clock, clock inhibit and serial input; high allows shifting; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 2 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising; text: NORed with CLOCK INHIBIT; either may be used as the clock |
| 3 | E | input | text: parallel data input |
| 4 | F | input | text: parallel data input |
| 5 | G | input | text: parallel data input |
| 6 | H | input | text: parallel data input |
| 7 | QH' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | QH | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | SERIAL INPUT | input | - |
| 11 | A | input | text: parallel data input |
| 12 | B | input | text: parallel data input |
| 13 | C | input | text: parallel data input |
| 14 | D | input | text: parallel data input |
| 15 | CLOCK INHIBIT | input | active: high; text: high inhibits clocking; change it to high only while CLOCK is high |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SHIFT/LOAD | any output | low to high | - | 21 | 31 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| SHIFT/LOAD | any output | high to low | - | 27 | 40 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | any output | low to high | - | 16 | 24 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | any output | high to low | - | 21 | 31 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| H | QH | low to high | - | 11 | 17 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| H | QH | high to low | - | 24 | 36 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| H | QH' | low to high | - | 18 | 27 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| H | QH' | high to low | - | 18 | 27 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** max clock frequency CLOCK minMHz 20 typMHz 26; pulse width CLOCK minNs 25; pulse width SHIFT/LOAD minNs 15; setup CLOCK INHIBIT to CLOCK minNs 30; setup A..H to SHIFT/LOAD minNs 10; setup SERIAL INPUT to CLOCK minNs 20; setup SHIFT/LOAD to CLOCK minNs 45; hold any input to CLOCK minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SHIFT/LOAD is a double load: IIL -3.2 mA, IIH 80 uA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The exact reference edges of the setup times are in datasheet Figures 1 and 2, which were not read; the 'ref' fields follow the parameter names.

### 74181 - Arithmetic logic unit/function generator

- **Part:** 74181
- **Description:** Arithmetic logic unit/function generator
- **Formula:** F = function of A, B selected by S3..S0, M and Cn per Table 1 (active-low data) / Table 2 (active-high data), see state.rules; Cn+4 = carry out of the 4-bit arithmetic operation selected by S3..S0 with M low; P' = carry propagate of the 4-bit group (for the '182) ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 36 (PDF page 42)

![74181 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74181.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74181 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74181.png) | ![74181 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74181.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, SN54181/SN74181 (bulletin DL-S 7611831, Dec 1972, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-271 to 7-275 and 7-280 (PDF pages 270-274, 279) (figures for SN74181)
- **Formulas:**
  - `F = function of A, B selected by S3..S0, M and Cn per Table 1 (active-low data) / Table 2 (active-high data), see state.rules` - Each F output is one bit of the result chosen by the function table. The gate-level formula per bit is in the datasheet's functional block diagram (page 7-280) and was not transcribed.
  - `Cn+4 = carry out of the 4-bit arithmetic operation selected by S3..S0 with M low` - Ripple carry output. With active-high data (Table 2) a low at Cn+4' means carry. Used as a magnitude comparator in subtract mode, see the last rule in state.
  - `P' = carry propagate of the 4-bit group (for the '182)` - Group propagate output; feeds a P input of the SN74182.
  - `G' = carry generate of the 4-bit group (for the '182)` - Group generate output; feeds a G input of the SN74182.
  - `A=B = F0 AND F1 AND F2 AND F3` - Decoded from the function outputs (an AND of the four F lines in the block diagram); goes high (off) when all F outputs are high, i.e. A = B when the ALU is in subtract mode with Cn = H.
- **Stored state:** 0 bits, changes on combinational (no memory); this block holds the datasheet function tables
  - Table 1, ACTIVE-LOW DATA. Columns: S3 S2 S1 S0 \| M=H logic \| M=L, Cn=L (no carry) \| M=L, Cn=H (with carry)
  - L L L L \| F = A' \| F = A MINUS 1 \| F = A
  - L L L H \| F = (AB)' \| F = AB MINUS 1 \| F = AB
  - L L H L \| F = A' + B \| F = AB' MINUS 1 \| F = AB'
  - L L H H \| F = 1 \| F = MINUS 1 (2's COMP) \| F = ZERO
  - L H L L \| F = (A + B)' \| F = A PLUS (A + B') \| F = A PLUS (A + B') PLUS 1
  - L H L H \| F = B' \| F = AB PLUS (A + B') \| F = AB PLUS (A + B') PLUS 1
  - L H H L \| F = (A XOR B)' \| F = A MINUS B MINUS 1 \| F = A MINUS B
  - L H H H \| F = A + B' \| F = A + B' \| F = (A + B') PLUS 1
  - H L L L \| F = A'B \| F = A PLUS (A + B) \| F = A PLUS (A + B) PLUS 1
  - H L L H \| F = A XOR B \| F = A PLUS B \| F = A PLUS B PLUS 1
  - H L H L \| F = B \| F = AB' PLUS (A + B) \| F = AB' PLUS (A + B) PLUS 1
  - H L H H \| F = A + B \| F = (A + B) \| F = (A + B) PLUS 1
  - H H L L \| F = 0 \| F = A PLUS A (*) \| F = A PLUS A PLUS 1
  - H H L H \| F = AB' \| F = AB PLUS A \| F = AB PLUS A PLUS 1
  - H H H L \| F = AB \| F = AB' PLUS A \| F = AB' PLUS A PLUS 1
  - H H H H \| F = A \| F = A \| F = A PLUS 1
  - Table 2, ACTIVE-HIGH DATA. Columns: S3 S2 S1 S0 \| M=H logic \| M=L, Cn'=H (no carry) \| M=L, Cn'=L (with carry)
  - L L L L \| F = A' \| F = A \| F = A PLUS 1
  - L L L H \| F = (A + B)' \| F = A + B \| F = (A + B) PLUS 1
  - L L H L \| F = A'B \| F = A + B' \| F = (A + B') PLUS 1
  - L L H H \| F = 0 \| F = MINUS 1 (2's COMPL) \| F = ZERO
  - L H L L \| F = (AB)' \| F = A PLUS AB' \| F = A PLUS AB' PLUS 1
  - L H L H \| F = B' \| F = (A + B) PLUS AB' \| F = (A + B) PLUS AB' PLUS 1
  - L H H L \| F = A XOR B \| F = A MINUS B MINUS 1 \| F = A MINUS B
  - L H H H \| F = AB' \| F = AB' MINUS 1 \| F = AB'
  - H L L L \| F = A' + B \| F = A PLUS AB \| F = A PLUS AB PLUS 1
  - H L L H \| F = (A XOR B)' \| F = A PLUS B \| F = A PLUS B PLUS 1
  - H L H L \| F = B \| F = (A + B') PLUS AB \| F = (A + B') PLUS AB PLUS 1
  - H L H H \| F = AB \| F = AB MINUS 1 \| F = AB
  - H H L L \| F = 1 \| F = A PLUS A (*) \| F = A PLUS A PLUS 1
  - H H L H \| F = A + B' \| F = (A + B) PLUS A \| F = (A + B) PLUS A PLUS 1
  - H H H L \| F = A + B \| F = (A + B') PLUS A \| F = (A + B') PLUS A PLUS 1
  - H H H H \| F = A \| F = A MINUS 1 \| F = A
  - (*) Each bit is shifted to the next more significant position.
  - Comparator use (subtract mode S3..S0 = L,H,H,L): Cn=H, Cn+4=H -> A >= B (active-low data) / A <= B (active-high); Cn=H, Cn+4=L -> A < B / A > B; Cn=L, Cn+4=H -> A > B / A < B; Cn=L, Cn+4=L -> A <= B / A >= B

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | B0' | input | text: operand B bit 0 (B0 with active-high data) |
| 2 | A0' | input | text: operand A bit 0 (A0 with active-high data) |
| 3 | S3 | input | text: function select |
| 4 | S2 | input | text: function select |
| 5 | S1 | input | text: function select |
| 6 | S0 | input | text: function select |
| 7 | Cn | input | text: ripple carry input (Cn' with active-high data) |
| 8 | M | input | text: mode control: high = logic functions (internal carries disabled), low = arithmetic |
| 9 | F0' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | F1' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | F2' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | GND | ground | - |
| 13 | F3' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | A=B | output | outputType: open-collector; text: comparator output; can be wire-ANDed |
| 15 | P' | output | outputType: totem-pole; text: carry propagate output for the '182 (X with active-high data) |
| 16 | Cn+4 | output | outputType: totem-pole; text: ripple carry output (Cn+4' with active-high data) |
| 17 | G' | output | outputType: totem-pole; text: carry generate output for the '182 (Y with active-high data) |
| 18 | B3' | input | - |
| 19 | A3' | input | - |
| 20 | B2' | input | - |
| 21 | A2' | input | - |
| 22 | B1' | input | - |
| 23 | A1' | input | - |
| 24 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Cn | Cn+4 | low to high | - | 12 | 18 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| Cn | Cn+4 | high to low | - | 13 | 19 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A or B | Cn+4 | low to high | - | 28 | 43 | M 0 V, S0=S3=4.5 V, S1=S2=0 V (SUM mode); CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, 25 C |
| A or B | Cn+4 | high to low | - | 27 | 41 | SUM mode |
| A or B | Cn+4 | low to high | - | 35 | 50 | M 0 V, S0=S3=0 V, S1=S2=4.5 V (DIFF mode) |
| A or B | Cn+4 | high to low | - | 33 | 50 | DIFF mode |
| Cn | F | low to high | - | 13 | 19 | M 0 V (SUM or DIFF mode) |
| Cn | F | high to low | - | 12 | 18 | M 0 V (SUM or DIFF mode) |
| A or B | G' | low to high | - | 13 | 19 | SUM mode |
| A or B | G' | high to low | - | 13 | 19 | SUM mode |
| A or B | G' | low to high | - | 17 | 25 | DIFF mode |
| A or B | G' | high to low | - | 17 | 25 | DIFF mode |
| A or B | P' | low to high | - | 13 | 19 | SUM mode |
| A or B | P' | high to low | - | 17 | 25 | SUM mode |
| A or B | P' | low to high | - | 17 | 25 | DIFF mode |
| A or B | P' | high to low | - | 17 | 25 | DIFF mode |
| Ai or Bi | Fi | low to high | - | 28 | 42 | SUM mode |
| Ai or Bi | Fi | high to low | - | 21 | 32 | SUM mode |
| Ai or Bi | Fi | low to high | - | 32 | 48 | DIFF mode |
| Ai or Bi | Fi | high to low | - | 23 | 34 | DIFF mode |
| Ai or Bi | Fi | low to high | - | 32 | 48 | M 4.5 V (logic mode) |
| Ai or Bi | Fi | high to low | - | 23 | 34 | M 4.5 V (logic mode) |
| A or B | A=B | low to high | - | 35 | 50 | DIFF mode |
| A or B | A=B | high to low | - | 32 | 48 | DIFF mode |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA; output may be pulled up to 5.5 V
- **Electrical notes:** SN74181: VCC 4.75-5.25 V; IOH -800 uA for all outputs except A=B; IOL 16 mA. A=B is open collector: IOH max 250 uA at VOH 5.5 V (the 5.5 V is that test condition; no separate pull-up limit is printed). Input loads: IIL mode -1.6 mA, any A or B -4.8, any S -6.4, carry -8 mA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Gate-level formulas for F0-F3, Cn+4, P' and G' were not transcribed from the functional block diagram (page 7-280); behaviour is given only by the function tables.; No separate maximum pull-up voltage for the open-collector A=B output beyond the 5.5 V IOH test condition.; No minimum propagation delays printed.
- **Notes:** Pin 2/1/23/... names are for active-low data (Table 1); with active-high data the same pins are A0, B0, ... F0..F3, Cn', Cn+4', X (pin 15), Y (pin 17) and Table 2 applies. Subtraction is 1's complement plus forced carry.

### 74182 - Look-ahead carry generator

- **Part:** 74182
- **Description:** Look-ahead carry generator
- **Formula:** Cn+x = G0 OR (P0 AND Cn); Cn+y = G1 OR (P1 AND G0) OR (P1 AND P0 AND Cn); Cn+z = G2 OR (P2 AND G1) OR (P2 AND P1 AND G0) OR (P2 AND P1 AND P0 AND Cn) ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 36 (PDF page 42)

![74182 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74182.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74182 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74182.png) | ![74182 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74182.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, SN54182/SN74182 (bulletin DL-S 7611823, Dec 1972, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-282 to 7-284 (PDF pages 281-283) (figures for SN74182)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Cn+x = G0 OR (P0 AND Cn)` - Datasheet equation, with G0 = NOT G0', P0 = NOT P0'. Function table: high when G0' low, or P0' low and Cn high; low otherwise.
  - `Cn+y = G1 OR (P1 AND G0) OR (P1 AND P0 AND Cn)` - Datasheet equation (G = NOT G', P = NOT P').
  - `Cn+z = G2 OR (P2 AND G1) OR (P2 AND P1 AND G0) OR (P2 AND P1 AND P0 AND Cn)` - Datasheet equation (G = NOT G', P = NOT P').
  - `G' = NOT (G3 OR (P3 AND G2) OR (P3 AND P2 AND G1) OR (P3 AND P2 AND P1 AND G0))` - Datasheet: G' = (G3 + P3G2 + P3P2G1 + P3P2P1G0)'. Low when the group generates a carry.
  - `P' = NOT (P3 AND P2 AND P1 AND P0)` - Low only when P3', P2', P1', P0' are all low.

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | G1' | input | text: carry generate input, active low (Y1) |
| 2 | P1' | input | text: carry propagate input, active low (X1) |
| 3 | G0' | input | text: carry generate input, active low (alternative name Y0) |
| 4 | P0' | input | text: carry propagate input, active low (X0) |
| 5 | G3' | input | text: carry generate input, active low (Y3) |
| 6 | P3' | input | text: carry propagate input, active low (X3) |

| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | Cn+z | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | G' | output | outputType: totem-pole; text: carry generate output, active low (Y) |
| 11 | Cn+y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | Cn+x | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | Cn | input | text: carry input |
| 14 | G2' | input | text: carry generate input, active low (Y2) |
| 15 | P2' | input | text: carry propagate input, active low (X2) |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| any | any | low to high | - | 11 | 17 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm; the datasheet gives one figure for all paths |
| any | any | high to low | - | 15 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm; the datasheet gives one figure for all paths |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74182 recommended: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, IOH -800 uA, IOL 16 mA, TA 0-70 C. Inputs load unequally: IIL Cn -3.2 mA, P3' -4.8, P2' -6.4, P0'/P1'/G3' -8, G0'/G2' -14.4, G1' -16 mA (several standard loads).
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** No minimum propagation delays printed (path-delay function uses 0 ns).
- **Notes:** The same circuit serves active-high data with the alternative names Y0-Y3, X0-X3, Cn', Y, X: Cn+x' = (Y0(X0 + Cn))' etc., as printed on page 7-282.

### 74191 - Synchronous up/down counter

- **Part:** 74191
- **Description:** Synchronous up/down counter
- **Formula:** QA..QD = Q[0..3]; MAX/MIN = (D/U' = L AND Q = 15) OR (D/U' = H AND Q = 0); RIPPLE CLOCK' = NOT (MAX/MIN AND G' = L AND CLOCK = L) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 37 (PDF page 43)

![74191 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74191.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74191 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74191.png) | ![74191 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74191.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, SN54190/SN54191/SN74190/SN74191 (bulletin DL-S 7611865, Dec 1972, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-296, 7-297, 7-299 to 7-301 (PDF pages 295, 296, 298-300) (figures for SN74191)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA..QD = Q[0..3]` - The four counter bits (QA least significant).
  - `MAX/MIN = (D/U' = L AND Q = 15) OR (D/U' = H AND Q = 0)` - High-level pulse about one full clock cycle long when the counter overflows (counting up) or underflows (counting down); the timing diagram shows it high at 15 counting up and at 0 counting down.
  - `RIPPLE CLOCK' = NOT (MAX/MIN AND G' = L AND CLOCK = L)` - Low-level pulse equal in width to the low part of the clock when an overflow or underflow condition exists (datasheet wording); used to cascade into the next counter's enable or clock.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - LOAD' low: Q = D,C,B,A at once, independent of the clock (asynchronous)
  - G' high: counting inhibited, Q holds
  - G' low, D/U' low: Q = Q + 1 (mod 16) at the rising clock edge
  - G' low, D/U' high: Q = Q - 1 (mod 16) at the rising clock edge
  - Change G' and D/U' only while CLOCK is high

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | B | input | text: data input (preset) |
| 2 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 3 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 4 | G' | input | text: count enable, active low: high inhibits counting; change it only while CLOCK is high |
| 5 | D/U' | input | text: down/up: low counts up, high counts down |
| 6 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | D | input | text: data input (preset) |
| 10 | C | input | text: data input (preset) |
| 11 | LOAD' | input | active: low; text: low: QA..QD take A..D at once, independent of the clock; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 12 | MAX/MIN | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | RIPPLE CLOCK' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 15 | A | input | text: data input (preset) |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LOAD' | QA..QD | low to high | - | 22 | 33 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| LOAD' | QA..QD | high to low | - | 33 | 50 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A..D | QA..QD | low to high | - | 14 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A..D | QA..QD | high to low | - | 35 | 50 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | RIPPLE CLOCK' | low to high | - | 13 | 20 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | RIPPLE CLOCK' | high to low | - | 16 | 24 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | QA..QD | low to high | - | 16 | 24 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | QA..QD | high to low | - | 24 | 36 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | MAX/MIN | low to high | - | 28 | 42 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | MAX/MIN | high to low | - | 37 | 52 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| D/U' | RIPPLE CLOCK' | low to high | - | 30 | 45 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| D/U' | RIPPLE CLOCK' | high to low | - | 30 | 45 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| D/U' | MAX/MIN | low to high | - | 21 | 33 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| D/U' | MAX/MIN | high to low | - | 22 | 33 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** maxClockFrequency CLOCK minMHz 20 typMHz 25; pulseWidth CLOCK minNs 25; pulseWidth LOAD' minNs 35; setup A..D to LOAD' minNs 20; hold A..D to LOAD' minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74191: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, TA 0-70 C. Enable input IIL -4.8 mA (three loads), other inputs -1.6 mA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The exact gate logic of MAX/MIN and RIPPLE CLOCK' is read from the description and timing diagram, not transcribed from the block diagram.; Setup/hold reference edge for the data inputs is in datasheet figures 1 and 2, which were not read; recorded here as relative to LOAD'.; No minimum propagation delays printed.
- **Notes:** Master-slave flip-flops triggered on the low-to-high clock transition.

### 74193 - Up/down binary counter

- **Part:** 74193
- **Description:** Up/down binary counter
- **Formula:** QA..QD = Q[0..3]; CARRY' = NOT (Q = 15 AND UP = L); BORROW' = NOT (Q = 0 AND DOWN = L) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 37 (PDF page 43)

![74193 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74193.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74193 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74193.png) | ![74193 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74193.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, SN54192/SN54193/SN74192/SN74193 (bulletin DL-S 7611828, Dec 1972, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-306, 7-311, 7-314 (PDF pages 305, 310, 313) (figures for SN74193)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA..QD = Q[0..3]` - The four counter bits (QA least significant).
  - `CARRY' = NOT (Q = 15 AND UP = L)` - Datasheet: the carry output produces a pulse equal in width to the count-up input when an overflow condition exists. Feeds the next counter's UP input.
  - `BORROW' = NOT (Q = 0 AND DOWN = L)` - Datasheet: the borrow output produces a pulse equal in width to the count-down input when the counter underflows. Feeds the next counter's DOWN input.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of UP or DOWN
  - CLEAR high: Q = 0 at once, overrides count and load
  - LOAD' low (CLEAR low): Q = D,C,B,A at once
  - UP rising while DOWN high: Q = Q + 1 (mod 16)
  - DOWN rising while UP high: Q = Q - 1 (mod 16)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | B | input | text: data input |
| 2 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 3 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 4 | DOWN | clock | edge: rising; text: count-down clock; the other count input must be high |
| 5 | UP | clock | edge: rising; text: count-up clock; the other count input must be high |
| 6 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | D | input | text: data input |
| 10 | C | input | text: data input |
| 11 | LOAD' | input | active: low; text: low: QA..QD take A..D, independent of the count pulses; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 12 | CARRY' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | BORROW' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | CLEAR | input | active: high; text: high forces all outputs low, independent of count and load; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 15 | A | input | text: data input |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UP | CARRY' | low to high | - | 17 | 26 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| UP | CARRY' | high to low | - | 16 | 24 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| DOWN | BORROW' | low to high | - | 16 | 24 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| DOWN | BORROW' | high to low | - | 16 | 24 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| UP or DOWN | QA..QD | low to high | - | 25 | 38 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| UP or DOWN | QA..QD | high to low | - | 31 | 47 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| LOAD' | QA..QD | low to high | - | 27 | 40 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| LOAD' | QA..QD | high to low | - | 29 | 40 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLEAR | QA..QD | high to low | - | 22 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** maxClockFrequency UP/DOWN minMHz 25 typMHz 32; pulseWidth any input minNs 20; setup A..D to LOAD' rising minNs 20; hold A..D to LOAD' rising minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74193: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, IOH -400 uA, IOL 16 mA, TA 0-70 C.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** CARRY'/BORROW' formulas are from the description text, not transcribed from the logic diagram.; No minimum propagation delays printed.
- **Notes:** Master-slave flip-flops triggered by a low-to-high transition of either count input.

### 74195 - 4-bit parallel-access shift register

- **Part:** 74195
- **Description:** 4-bit parallel-access shift register
- **Formula:** QA..QD = Q[0..3]; QD' = NOT QD (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 38 (PDF page 44)

![74195 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74195.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74195 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74195.png) | ![74195 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74195.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, SN54195/SN74195 (bulletin DL-S 7611820, Mar 1974, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-324, 7-327 (PDF pages 323, 326) (figures for SN74195)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA..QD = Q[0..3]` - Register bits.
  - `QD' = NOT QD` - Complement output of the last stage.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR' low: QA..QD = L, QD' = H at once
  - CLEAR' high, SHIFT/LOAD' low, clock rising: QA..QD = A,B,C,D (serial inputs ignored)
  - CLEAR' high, SHIFT/LOAD' high, CLOCK low: no change
  - Shift (SHIFT/LOAD' high, clock rising): QB=QAn, QC=QBn, QD=QCn, and QA from J,K':
  -   J=L, K'=H: QA = QAn (no change)
  -   J=L, K'=L: QA = L
  -   J=H, K'=H: QA = H
  -   J=H, K'=L: QA = NOT QAn (toggle)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | CLEAR' | input | active: low; text: direct overriding clear; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 2 | J | input | text: serial input to first stage |
| 3 | K' | input | text: serial input to first stage (inverted K) |
| 4 | A | input | text: parallel data |
| 5 | B | input | text: parallel data |
| 6 | C | input | text: parallel data |
| 7 | D | input | text: parallel data |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | SHIFT/LOAD' | input | text: low: parallel load at the clock edge; high: shift QA toward QD |
| 10 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 11 | QD' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 15 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLEAR' | QA..QD | high to low | - | 19 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | QA..QD, QD' | low to high | - | 14 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | QA..QD, QD' | high to low | - | 17 | 26 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** maxClockFrequency CLOCK minMHz 30 typMHz 39; pulseWidth CLOCK minNs 16; pulseWidth CLEAR' minNs 12; setup SHIFT/LOAD' to CLOCK minNs 25; setup J, K', A..D to CLOCK minNs 20; setup CLEAR' inactive to CLOCK minNs 25; release SHIFT/LOAD' to CLOCK maxNs 10; hold J, K', A..D to CLOCK minNs 0

### 74198 - 8-bit shift register

- **Part:** 74198
- **Description:** 8-bit shift register
- **Formula:** QA..QH = Q[0..7] (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 38 (PDF page 44)

![74198 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74198.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74198 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74198.png) | ![74198 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74198.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, SN54198/SN54199/SN74198/SN74199 (bulletin DL-S 7611841, Dec 1972, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-338, 7-339, 7-343, 7-344 (PDF pages 337, 338, 342, 343) (figures for SN74198)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA..QH = Q[0..7]` - Register bits.
- **Stored state:** 8 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR' low: QA..QH = L at once
  - CLOCK low (no edge): hold
  - S1=H, S0=H: parallel load QA..QH = A..H at the clock edge
  - S1=L, S0=H: shift right (QA toward QH): QA = SR SER, QB = QAn, ..., QH = QGn
  - S1=H, S0=L: shift left (QH toward QA): QH = SL SER, QG = QHn, ..., QA = QBn
  - S1=L, S0=L: clock inhibited, hold
  - Change the mode inputs only while CLOCK is high

### 74S258 - Quadruple 2-line-to-1-line data selector multiplexer with tri-state outputs

- **Part:** 74S258
- **Description:** Quadruple 2-line-to-1-line data selector multiplexer with tri-state outputs
- **Formula:** nY = NOT ((SELECT' AND nA) OR (SELECT AND nB)), Z when G' high (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 40 (PDF page 46)

![74S258 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74S258.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74S258 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74S258.png) | ![74S258 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74S258.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book 2nd ed. 1976, SN54S257/SN54S258/SN74S257/SN74S258 (bulletin DL-S 7611734, Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-372, 7-375 (PDF pages 371, 374) (figures for SN74S258)
- **Formulas:**
  - `nY = NOT ((SELECT' AND nA) OR (SELECT AND nB)), Z when G' high` - Inverting: with G' low, Y is the complement of A (SELECT low) or of B (SELECT high); G' high puts all outputs in high impedance.

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | SELECT | input | text: low selects A inputs, high selects B inputs |
| 2 | 1A | input | - |
| 3 | 1B | input | - |
| 4 | 1Y | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: 15 |
| 5 | 2A | input | - |
| 6 | 2B | input | - |
| 7 | 2Y | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: 15 |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | 3Y | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: 15 |
| 10 | 3B | input | - |
| 11 | 3A | input | - |
| 12 | 4Y | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: 15 |
| 13 | 4B | input | - |
| 14 | 4A | input | - |
| 15 | G' | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; text: output control; controls: 4, 7, 9, 12 |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A or B | Y | low to high | - | 4 | 6 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| A or B | Y | high to low | - | 4 | 6 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| SELECT | Y | low to high | - | 8 | 12 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| SELECT | Y | high to low | - | 7.5 | 12 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| G' | Y | PZH | - | 13 | 19.5 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| G' | Y | PZL | - | 14 | 21 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| G' | Y | PHZ | - | 5.5 | 8.5 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 5 pF |
| G' | Y | PLZ | - | 9 | 14 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 5 pF |

- **Output current:** low 20 mA, high -6.5 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74S258: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, IOH -6.5 mA, IOL 20 mA, TA 0-70 C. Off-state current +/-50 uA. S input IIL -4 mA, others -2 mA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** No minimum propagation delays printed.
- **Notes:** Function table rows: G'=H -> Z; G'=L, S=L, A=L -> H; A=H -> L; S=H, B=L -> H; B=H -> L. Output disable is faster than enable, by design.

### 74S280 - 9-bit odd/even parity generator/checker

- **Part:** 74S280
- **Description:** 9-bit odd/even parity generator/checker
- **Formula:** SUM EVEN = NOT (A XOR B XOR C XOR D XOR E XOR F XOR G XOR H XOR I); SUM ODD = A XOR B XOR C XOR D XOR E XOR F XOR G XOR H XOR I (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 41 (PDF page 47)

![74S280 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74S280.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74S280 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74S280.png) | ![74S280 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74S280.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book 2nd ed. 1976, SN54LS280/SN54S280/SN74LS280/SN74S280 (bulletin DL-S 7611829, Dec 1972, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-406, 7-408 (PDF pages 405, 407) (figures for SN74S280)
- **Formulas:**
  - `SUM EVEN = NOT (A XOR B XOR C XOR D XOR E XOR F XOR G XOR H XOR I)` - High when an even number (0,2,4,6,8) of inputs A-I are high.
  - `SUM ODD = A XOR B XOR C XOR D XOR E XOR F XOR G XOR H XOR I` - High when an odd number (1,3,5,7,9) of inputs A-I are high.

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | G | input | - |
| 2 | H | input | - |
| 3 | NC | nc | text: no internal connection (lets it replace a '180) |
| 4 | I | input | - |
| 5 | SUM EVEN | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 6 | SUM ODD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | A | input | - |
| 9 | B | input | - |
| 10 | C | input | - |
| 11 | D | input | - |
| 12 | E | input | - |
| 13 | F | input | - |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| any data | SUM EVEN | low to high | - | 14 | 21 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 180 ohm |
| any data | SUM EVEN | high to low | - | 11.5 | 18 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 180 ohm |
| any data | SUM ODD | low to high | - | 14 | 21 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 180 ohm |
| any data | SUM ODD | high to low | - | 11.5 | 18 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 180 ohm |

- **Output current:** low 20 mA, high -1 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74S280: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, TA 0-70 C. Inputs are one 74S load (IIL -2 mA).
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** No minimum propagation delays printed.

### 74298 - Quadruple 2-input multiplexer with storage

- **Part:** 74298
- **Description:** Quadruple 2-input multiplexer with storage
- **Formula:** QA..QD = stored bits (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 42 (PDF page 48)

![74298 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74298.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74298 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74298.png) | ![74298 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74298.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book 2nd ed. 1976, SN54298/SN74298 (bulletin DL-S 7611747, Mar 1974, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-432, 7-434 (PDF pages 431, 433) (figures for SN74298)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA..QD = stored bits` - Outputs of the four flip-flops.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on falling edge of CLOCK
  - WORD SELECT low: QA..QD = A1,B1,C1,D1 at the falling clock edge
  - WORD SELECT high: QA..QD = A2,B2,C2,D2 at the falling clock edge
  - CLOCK high (no falling edge): hold

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | B2 | input | - |
| 2 | A2 | input | - |
| 3 | A1 | input | - |
| 4 | B1 | input | - |
| 5 | C2 | input | - |
| 6 | D2 | input | - |
| 7 | D1 | input | - |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | C1 | input | - |
| 10 | WORD SELECT | input | text: low: word 1 (A1,B1,C1,D1); high: word 2 (A2,B2,C2,D2) |
| 11 | CLOCK | clock | edge: falling |
| 12 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 15 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLOCK | QA..QD | low to high | - | 18 | 27 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | QA..QD | high to low | - | 21 | 32 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** pulseWidth CLOCK minNs 20; setup data to CLOCK falling minNs 15; setup WORD SELECT to CLOCK falling minNs 25; hold data to CLOCK falling minNs 5; hold WORD SELECT to CLOCK falling minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74298: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, TA 0-70 C.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The switching table names no input for its single tPLH/tPHL pair; recorded as clock to output (the only path that drives the outputs).; No maximum clock frequency printed for the SN74298.; No minimum propagation delays printed.

### 74393 - Dual 4-bit binary ripple counter

- **Part:** 74393
- **Description:** Dual 4-bit binary ripple counter
- **Formula:** 1QA..1QD = counter 1 bits; 2QA..2QD = counter 2 bits (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 42 (PDF page 48)

![74393 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74393.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74393 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74393.png) | ![74393 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74393.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book 2nd ed. 1976, SN54390/SN54393/SN74390/SN74393 (bulletin DL-S 7612099, Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-489, 7-492, 7-493 (PDF pages 488, 491, 492) (figures for SN74393)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1QA..1QD = counter 1 bits` - QA least significant.
  - `2QA..2QD = counter 2 bits` - QA least significant.
- **Stored state:** 8 bits, changes on falling edge of 1A / 2A (each counter separately)
  - nCLEAR high: nQA..nQD = L at once (positive logic note: high input to clear resets all four outputs low)
  - nCLEAR low, falling edge of nA: count = count + 1 (mod 16); ripple: QA toggles on the A edge, QB on QA falling, QC on QB falling, QD on QC falling

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1A | clock | edge: falling; text: count input of counter 1 |
| 2 | 1CLEAR | input | active: high; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 3 | 1QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 4 | 1QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 5 | 1QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 6 | 1QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | 2QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 9 | 2QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | 2QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | 2QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | 2CLEAR | input | active: high; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 13 | 2A | clock | edge: falling; text: count input of counter 2 |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A | QA | low to high | - | 12 | 20 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A | QA | high to low | - | 13 | 20 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A | QD | low to high | - | 40 | 60 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A | QD | high to low | - | 40 | 60 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLEAR | any Q | high to low | - | 24 | 39 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** maxClockFrequency A minMHz 25 typMHz 35; pulseWidth A minNs 20; pulseWidth CLEAR minNs 20; setup CLEAR inactive to A falling minNs 25
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74393: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, TA 0-70 C. A input IIL -3.2 mA, clear -1 mA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** '393 delays from A to QB and QC are not printed (only to QA and QD).; No minimum propagation delays printed.
- **Notes:** Ripple-counter rule (QB clocked by QA etc.) is taken from the internal connection shown in the pin diagram and the 'ripple' description; the 7-489 page shows A feeding the first stage of each 4-bit counter.

### 8097 - Tri-state hex buffer

- **Part:** 8097
- **Description:** Tri-state hex buffer
- **Formula:** Y = A
- **Manual page:** 47 (PDF page 53)

![8097 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/8097.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![8097 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-8097.png) | ![8097 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-8097.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1976 TTL Databook, DM70/DM8095,L95,96,L96,97,L97,98,L98 TRI-STATE Hex Buffers](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1976_National_TTL_Databook.pdf) p. pdf 352-353 (printed 3-7, 3-8) (figures for DM8097)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Yn = An when G1' ; Z when G1 high` - Y1..Y4 give the same level as A1..A4 while G1 is low; they float (high impedance) while G1 is high.
  - `Yn = An when G2' ; Z when G2 high` - Y5, Y6 give the same level as A5, A6 while G2 is low; they float while G2 is high.

### 8098 - Tri-state hex inverter

- **Part:** 8098
- **Description:** Tri-state hex inverter
- **Formula:** Y = NOT A
- **Manual page:** 48 (PDF page 54)

### 8160 - 6-bit comparator

- **Part:** 8160
- **Description:** 6-bit comparator
- **Formula:** Y = STROBE OR ((A1 XNOR B1) AND (A2 XNOR B2) AND (A3 XNOR B3) AND (A4 XNOR B4) AND (A5 XNOR B5) AND (A6 XNOR B6)) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 49 (PDF page 55)

![8160 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/8160.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![8160 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-8160.png) | ![8160 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-8160.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1976 TTL Databook, DM71/DM8130,60 Magnitude Comparators](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1976_National_TTL_Databook.pdf) p. pdf 362-363 (printed 3-17, 3-18) (figures for DM8160)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Y = STROBE OR ((A1 XNOR B1) AND (A2 XNOR B2) AND (A3 XNOR B3) AND (A4 XNOR B4) AND (A5 XNOR B5) AND (A6 XNOR B6))` - Y is high (output transistor off) when the two 6-bit words are equal, or when STROBE is high. Y is low when STROBE is low and the words differ. Open collector, so several can be wired together.

| 12 | B5 | input | - |
| 13 | A5 | input | - |
| 14 | B6 | input | - |
| 15 | A6 | input | - |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high - mA; output may be pulled up to 5.5 V
- **Electrical notes:** Open collector: IOH 100 uA max leakage at VOH 5.5 V.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** No minimum delays printed.; The pin-7 label in the diagram carries a bar, but the description and truth table say a HIGH strobe forces Y high; the formula follows the truth table.
- **Notes:** National calls it a "magnitude comparator" but it only tests for equality.

### 8551 - Tri-state quad D flip flop (see 74173)

- **Part:** 8551
- **Description:** Tri-state quad D flip flop (see 74173)
- **Formula:** Qn = stored bit n when M' AND N'; Z when M OR N (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 50 (PDF page 56)

![8551 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/8551.png)

- **Drawing:** the manual prints no drawing for this part, only a reference to another part.

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1976 TTL Databook, DM75/DM8551,L51 TRI-STATE 4-Bit D Type Registers](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1976_National_TTL_Databook.pdf) p. pdf 407-408 (printed 3-62, 3-63) (figures for DM8551)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Qn = stored bit n when M' AND N'; Z when M OR N` - Each Q shows its flip-flop while M and N are both low; if either is high the outputs float. The flip-flops keep working while the outputs float.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR high: all four bits = 0 at once
  - CLEAR low, G1 and G2 both low: bit n = Dn at the rising CLOCK edge
  - CLEAR low, G1 or G2 high: bits hold at the clock edge
  - CLOCK low: bits hold

| 4 | Q2 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: M,N |
| 5 | Q3 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: M,N |
| 6 | Q4 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: M,N |
| 7 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | G1 | input | - |
| 10 | G2 | input | - |
| 11 | D4 | input | - |
| 12 | D3 | input | - |
| 13 | D2 | input | - |
| 14 | D1 | input | - |
| 15 | CLEAR | input | active: high; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| CLOCK | Q | low to high | - | 16 | 25 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 50 pF, RL 400 ohm |

### 8553 - Tri-state 8 bit latch

- **Part:** 8553
- **Description:** Tri-state 8 bit latch
- **Formula:** IOn = latch n when ENABLE' AND READ1' AND READ2' AND WRITE1 AND WRITE2; otherwise Z (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 50 (PDF page 56)

![8553 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/8553.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![8553 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-8553.png) | ![8553 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-8553.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1976 TTL Databook, DM75/DM8553 TRI-STATE 8-Bit Latches](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1976_National_TTL_Databook.pdf) p. pdf 415-416 (printed 3-70, 3-71) (figures for DM8553)
- **Formulas:**
  - `IOn = latch n when ENABLE' AND READ1' AND READ2' AND WRITE1 AND WRITE2; otherwise Z` - The I/O pins drive the stored byte only when ENABLE and both READ inputs are low and both WRITE inputs are high (read). Otherwise they float and act as inputs.
- **Stored state:** 8 bits, changes on level (transparent latch while writing)
  - CLEAR H, ENABLE L, READ L, WRITE H: enter L into all latches, bus shows L
  - CLEAR H, ENABLE L, READ L, WRITE L: enter L, bus Hi-Z
  - CLEAR L, ENABLE L, READ H, WRITE H: do nothing, bus Hi-Z
  - CLEAR L, ENABLE H: do nothing, bus Hi-Z
  - CLEAR L, ENABLE L, WRITE L: write, latches take the bus levels
  - CLEAR L, ENABLE L, READ L, WRITE H: read, bus shows the latches

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | READ1 | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; controls: 4, 5, 6, 7, 9, 10, 11, 12 |
| 2 | ENABLE | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; controls: 4, 5, 6, 7, 9, 10, 11, 12 |
| 3 | CLEAR | input | active: high; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 4 | IO1 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ENABLE,READ1,READ2; text: bidirectional: data input when floating, latch output when read |
| 5 | IO2 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ENABLE,READ1,READ2; text: bidirectional: data input when floating, latch output when read |
| 6 | IO3 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ENABLE,READ1,READ2; text: bidirectional: data input when floating, latch output when read |
| 7 | IO4 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ENABLE,READ1,READ2; text: bidirectional: data input when floating, latch output when read |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | IO5 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ENABLE,READ1,READ2; text: bidirectional: data input when floating, latch output when read |
| 10 | IO6 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ENABLE,READ1,READ2; text: bidirectional: data input when floating, latch output when read |
| 11 | IO7 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ENABLE,READ1,READ2; text: bidirectional: data input when floating, latch output when read |
| 12 | IO8 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ENABLE,READ1,READ2; text: bidirectional: data input when floating, latch output when read |
| 13 | WRITE2 | input | active: low |
| 14 | WRITE1 | input | active: low |
| 15 | READ2 | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; controls: 4, 5, 6, 7, 9, 10, 11, 12 |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLEAR | IO | high to low | - | 21 | 32 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 50 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| ENABLE/READ | IO | PZH | - | 22 | 33 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 50 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** pulseWidth CLEAR minNs 15 typNs 10; pulseWidth WRITE minNs 40 typNs 28; setup IO (high level) to WRITE minNs 20 typNs 14; setup IO (low level) to WRITE minNs 36 typNs 26; hold IO (high level) to WRITE minNs -15 typNs -26; hold IO (low level) to WRITE minNs -8 typNs -14
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -5.2 mA
- **Electrical notes:** DM85 column.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The reference signal for data setup/hold (taken as WRITE) is not named in the text read; the timing waveform was not read.; In the truth table READ and WRITE mean "both READ inputs" / "both WRITE inputs"; behaviour with the two READ (or two WRITE) pins at different levels is not stated.; No minimum delays printed.
- **Notes:** The IO pins are both inputs and three-state outputs; given role output with a text note.

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |

| ![8599 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-8599.png) | ![8599 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-8599.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1976 TTL Databook, DM75/DM8599 TRI-STATE 64-Bit Random Access Memories](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1976_National_TTL_Databook.pdf) p. pdf 472-475 (printed 3-127..3-130) (figures for DM8599)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Sn = NOT mem[A3..A0][n] when ME' AND WE; Z when ME high` - With ME low and WE high, S1..S4 show the COMPLEMENT of the 4-bit word stored at address A3..A0. With ME high the outputs float.
- **Stored state:** 64 bits, changes on level: ME low and WE low
  - ME L, WE L: D1..D4 written into word A3..A0
  - ME L, WE H: read, outputs show the complement of the stored word
  - ME H: hold, outputs Hi-Z

|---|---|---|---|
